<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en" data-theme="dark">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
<title>Enterprise SDLC Tracking Dashboard</title>
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;500;600;700;800&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;500;600;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
<script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/Chart.js/4.4.1/chart.umd.min.js"></script>
<script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/xlsx/0.18.5/xlsx.full.min.js"></script>
<style>
/* ============================================================
   DESIGN TOKENS  — dark enterprise BI theme
   ============================================================ */
:root{
  --bg:#0d1017; --bg-2:#12161f; --panel:#161b26; --panel-2:#1b2230;
  --elev:#212a3a; --line:#28313f; --line-2:#333f52;
  --ink:#e7edf5; --ink-2:#a3b1c4; --ink-3:#6b7889;
  --brand:#2de1c2; --brand-dim:#17a892; --brand-glow:rgba(45,225,194,.14);
  --violet:#9d8bff; --blue:#5aa9ff; --amber:#f0b429; --green:#39d98a;
  --red:#ff5c6c; --orange:#ff924d; --pink:#ff6fb5;
  --ok:#39d98a; --warn:#f0b429; --danger:#ff5c6c; --info:#5aa9ff; --neutral:#6b7889;
  --shadow:0 10px 30px -12px rgba(0,0,0,.6);
  --radius:14px; --radius-sm:9px;
  --mono:'JetBrains Mono',ui-monospace,SFMono-Regular,Menlo,monospace;
  --sans:'Inter',system-ui,-apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,sans-serif;
  --rail:236px; --topbar:60px;
}
*{box-sizing:border-box}
html,body{margin:0;height:100%}
body{background:var(--bg);color:var(--ink);font-family:var(--sans);font-size:14px;
  -webkit-font-smoothing:antialiased;line-height:1.45;overflow:hidden}
::-webkit-scrollbar{width:10px;height:10px}
::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb{background:var(--line-2);border-radius:20px;border:2px solid var(--bg)}
::-webkit-scrollbar-track{background:transparent}
a{color:var(--brand);text-decoration:none}
button{font-family:inherit;cursor:pointer}
.mono{font-family:var(--mono);font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
.muted{color:var(--ink-2)} .dim{color:var(--ink-3)}

/* ============================================================ LAYOUT */
#app{display:grid;grid-template-columns:var(--rail) 1fr;grid-template-rows:var(--topbar) 1fr;
  grid-template-areas:"rail topbar" "rail main";height:100vh}
.rail{grid-area:rail;background:linear-gradient(180deg,#10141d,#0c0f16);border-right:1px solid var(--line);
  display:flex;flex-direction:column;overflow:hidden}
.topbar{grid-area:topbar;background:var(--bg-2);border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);
  display:flex;align-items:center;gap:14px;padding:0 20px;z-index:30}
.main{grid-area:main;overflow-y:auto;overflow-x:hidden;padding:22px 26px 60px;scroll-behavior:smooth}

/* ============================================================ RAIL */
.brand{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:11px;padding:16px 18px 14px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
.brand .logo{width:34px;height:34px;border-radius:9px;display:grid;place-items:center;flex-shrink:0;
  background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--brand),var(--brand-dim));color:#06231e;font-weight:800;font-size:16px;
  box-shadow:0 0 20px var(--brand-glow)}
.brand h1{font-size:14px;margin:0;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.2px}
.brand span{font-size:10.5px;color:var(--ink-3);letter-spacing:1.4px;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:600}
.nav{flex:1;overflow-y:auto;padding:10px 10px 20px}
.nav-group{font-size:10px;letter-spacing:1.5px;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);
  padding:14px 12px 6px;font-weight:700}
.nav-item{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:11px;padding:9px 12px;border-radius:10px;color:var(--ink-2);
  font-weight:500;font-size:13px;cursor:pointer;position:relative;margin:1px 0;transition:.14s;user-select:none}
.nav-item:hover{background:var(--panel);color:var(--ink)}
.nav-item.active{background:linear-gradient(90deg,var(--brand-glow),transparent);color:var(--ink)}
.nav-item.active::before{content:"";position:absolute;left:0;top:7px;bottom:7px;width:3px;border-radius:3px;
  background:var(--brand);box-shadow:0 0 10px var(--brand)}
.nav-item svg{width:17px;height:17px;flex-shrink:0;stroke-width:1.8}
.nav-item .badge{margin-left:auto;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10.5px;background:var(--elev);
  color:var(--ink-2);padding:1px 7px;border-radius:20px;font-weight:600}
.nav-item.active .badge{background:var(--brand);color:#06231e}
.rail-foot{padding:12px 16px;border-top:1px solid var(--line);font-size:10.5px;color:var(--ink-3)}

/* ============================================================ TOPBAR */
.crumb{font-size:13px;color:var(--ink-2);font-weight:500;white-space:nowrap}
.crumb b{color:var(--ink);font-weight:700}
.search{flex:1;max-width:420px;position:relative}
.search input{width:100%;background:var(--panel);border:1px solid var(--line);color:var(--ink);
  padding:8px 12px 8px 34px;border-radius:9px;font-size:13px;font-family:inherit;outline:none;transition:.15s}
.search input:focus{border-color:var(--brand-dim);box-shadow:0 0 0 3px var(--brand-glow)}
.search svg{position:absolute;left:10px;top:9px;width:15px;height:15px;color:var(--ink-3)}
.search-results{position:absolute;top:42px;left:0;right:0;background:var(--panel-2);border:1px solid var(--line-2);
  border-radius:11px;box-shadow:var(--shadow);max-height:60vh;overflow-y:auto;z-index:60;display:none}
.search-results.open{display:block}
.sr-item{padding:9px 13px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);cursor:pointer;display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center}
.sr-item:hover{background:var(--elev)}
.sr-item .tag{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10px;padding:1px 6px;border-radius:5px;font-weight:600;flex-shrink:0}
.sr-item .t{font-size:12.5px;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap}
.top-actions{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;margin-left:auto}
.tbtn{background:var(--panel);border:1px solid var(--line);color:var(--ink-2);padding:7px 11px;border-radius:8px;
  font-size:12px;font-weight:600;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;transition:.14s;white-space:nowrap}
.tbtn:hover{background:var(--elev);color:var(--ink);border-color:var(--line-2)}
.tbtn svg{width:14px;height:14px}
.tbtn.primary{background:var(--brand);color:#06231e;border-color:transparent}
.tbtn.primary:hover{filter:brightness(1.08)}
.asof{font-size:11px;color:var(--ink-3);display:flex;flex-direction:column;line-height:1.25;text-align:right}
.asof b{color:var(--ink-2);font-family:var(--mono);font-size:12px}

/* ============================================================ FILTER BAR */
.filterbar{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:9px;align-items:center;margin-bottom:20px}
.fsel{position:relative}
.fsel select,.fsel input{appearance:none;background:var(--panel);border:1px solid var(--line);color:var(--ink);
  padding:7px 30px 7px 12px;border-radius:8px;font-size:12.5px;font-family:inherit;font-weight:500;outline:none;cursor:pointer}
.fsel input{cursor:text;padding-right:12px}
.fsel select:focus,.fsel input:focus{border-color:var(--brand-dim)}
.fsel::after{content:"▾";position:absolute;right:11px;top:7px;color:var(--ink-3);pointer-events:none;font-size:11px}
.fsel.noarrow::after{content:""}
.fsel label{position:absolute;top:-7px;left:9px;font-size:9px;background:var(--bg);padding:0 5px;color:var(--ink-3);
  letter-spacing:.5px;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:700;white-space:nowrap;pointer-events:none;z-index:1}
.fsel input[type=date]{min-width:150px}
.fsel input[type=date]::-webkit-calendar-picker-indicator{filter:invert(.7);cursor:pointer}
.clearf{color:var(--ink-3);font-size:12px;font-weight:600;padding:7px 4px;background:none;border:none}
.clearf:hover{color:var(--brand)}
.chipcount{margin-left:auto;font-size:12px;color:var(--ink-2)}
.chipcount b{color:var(--ink);font-family:var(--mono)}

/* ============================================================ SECTION / GRID */
.view{display:none;animation:fade .3s ease}
.view.active{display:block}
@keyframes fade{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(6px)}to{opacity:1;transform:none}}
.page-head{display:flex;align-items:flex-end;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:18px;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:10px}
.page-head h2{font-size:21px;margin:0 0 3px;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:-.3px}
.page-head p{margin:0;color:var(--ink-3);font-size:12.5px}
.grid{display:grid;gap:14px}
.g-kpi{grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(210px,1fr))}
.g-2{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr)} .g-3{grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr)}
.g-4{grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr)}
.span-2{grid-column:span 2} .span-3{grid-column:span 3}
@media(max-width:1200px){.g-3,.g-4{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr)}.span-3{grid-column:span 2}}
.mt{margin-top:14px}

/* ============================================================ CARD */
.card{background:var(--panel);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:var(--radius);padding:16px 17px;position:relative}
.card-h{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:13px}
.card-h h3{font-size:13px;margin:0;font-weight:700;color:var(--ink);letter-spacing:.1px}
.card-h .sub{font-size:11px;color:var(--ink-3);font-weight:500}
.card-tools{display:flex;gap:6px}
.mini-tab{font-size:11px;padding:3px 9px;border-radius:6px;border:1px solid var(--line);background:transparent;
  color:var(--ink-3);font-weight:600}
.mini-tab.on{background:var(--elev);color:var(--ink);border-color:var(--line-2)}

/* KPI card */
.kpi{background:var(--panel);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:var(--radius);padding:15px 16px;
  position:relative;overflow:hidden;transition:.15s;cursor:default}
.kpi.click{cursor:pointer}
.kpi.click:hover{border-color:var(--line-2);transform:translateY(-2px);box-shadow:var(--shadow)}
.kpi .k-top{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:9px}
.kpi .k-label{font-size:11px;color:var(--ink-2);font-weight:600;letter-spacing:.3px;text-transform:uppercase}
.kpi .k-ic{width:30px;height:30px;border-radius:8px;display:grid;place-items:center;flex-shrink:0}
.kpi .k-ic svg{width:16px;height:16px}
.kpi .k-val{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:29px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:-1px;line-height:1}
.kpi .k-val small{font-size:15px;color:var(--ink-3);font-weight:500}
.kpi .k-sub{margin-top:8px;font-size:11.5px;color:var(--ink-3);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px}
.kpi .delta{font-weight:700;font-family:var(--mono)}
.kpi .accent{position:absolute;left:0;top:0;bottom:0;width:3px}

/* progress bar */
.pbar{height:7px;border-radius:20px;background:var(--elev);overflow:hidden;position:relative}
.pbar>span{position:absolute;left:0;top:0;bottom:0;border-radius:20px;transition:width .5s cubic-bezier(.2,.7,.3,1)}
.pbar.tall{height:9px}
.pwrap{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px}
.pwrap .pbar{flex:1}
.pwrap .pval{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:12px;font-weight:600;min-width:38px;text-align:right}

/* stacked bar */
.stack{display:flex;height:11px;border-radius:20px;overflow:hidden;background:var(--elev)}
.stack>span{transition:width .5s}
.legend{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:11px;margin-top:11px}
.legend .li{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;font-size:11.5px;color:var(--ink-2)}
.legend .dot{width:9px;height:9px;border-radius:3px;flex-shrink:0}
.legend .li b{color:var(--ink);font-family:var(--mono)}

/* ============================================================ TABLE */
.tablewrap{overflow-x:auto;border-radius:10px;border:1px solid var(--line)}
table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:12.5px}
thead th{background:var(--panel-2);text-align:left;padding:9px 12px;font-size:10.5px;letter-spacing:.6px;
  text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);font-weight:700;position:sticky;top:0;cursor:pointer;white-space:nowrap;
  border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
thead th:hover{color:var(--ink-2)}
thead th .arr{opacity:.5;font-size:9px;margin-left:3px}
tbody td{padding:9px 12px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);color:var(--ink-2);vertical-align:middle}
tbody tr{transition:background .1s}
tbody tr:hover{background:var(--panel-2)}
tbody tr.click{cursor:pointer}
tbody tr:last-child td{border-bottom:none}
td .code{font-family:var(--mono);color:var(--brand);font-weight:600;font-size:12px}
a.extlink{cursor:pointer;text-decoration:none} a.extlink:hover{text-decoration:underline;filter:brightness(1.15)}
td.tt{max-width:340px;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;color:var(--ink)}
.tnum{font-family:var(--mono);text-align:right}
.empty{padding:40px;text-align:center;color:var(--ink-3);font-size:13px}
.empty svg{width:34px;height:34px;opacity:.4;margin-bottom:10px}

/* ============================================================ PILLS / BADGES */
.pill{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;padding:2px 9px;border-radius:20px;font-size:11px;
  font-weight:600;white-space:nowrap;line-height:1.6}
.pill .d{width:6px;height:6px;border-radius:50%}
.pill.ok{background:rgba(57,217,138,.13);color:#5fe3a3}
.pill.warn{background:rgba(240,180,41,.14);color:#f5c651}
.pill.danger{background:rgba(255,92,108,.14);color:#ff8994}
.pill.info{background:rgba(90,169,255,.14);color:#84c0ff}
.pill.violet{background:rgba(157,139,255,.15);color:#b7a9ff}
.pill.neutral{background:rgba(107,120,137,.16);color:#9aa8ba}
.pill.orange{background:rgba(255,146,77,.15);color:#ffb182}
.sev-dot{width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;display:inline-block;margin-right:6px;vertical-align:middle}

/* ============================================================ GAUGE / DONUT holders */
.chart-box{position:relative;width:100%}
.chart-box.h200{height:200px} .chart-box.h240{height:240px} .chart-box.h280{height:280px}
.chart-box.h320{height:320px}
.donut-center{position:absolute;inset:0;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;
  pointer-events:none}
.donut-center .big{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:27px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:-1px}
.donut-center .lbl{font-size:10.5px;color:var(--ink-3);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:1px;font-weight:600}

/* gauge */
.gauge{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:4px}
.gauge svg{width:100%;max-width:230px;height:auto}
.gauge .gval{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:34px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:-1.5px}
.gauge .glbl{font-size:11px;color:var(--ink-3);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:1px;font-weight:600}

/* ============================================================ PIPELINE / FUNNEL */
.funnel{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px}
.fstage{display:grid;grid-template-columns:120px 1fr 46px;align-items:center;gap:11px}
.fstage .fn{font-size:11.5px;color:var(--ink-2);font-weight:600;text-align:right}
.fstage .fbar{height:24px;border-radius:6px;position:relative;background:var(--elev);overflow:hidden;cursor:pointer}
.fstage .fbar>span{position:absolute;left:0;top:0;bottom:0;border-radius:6px;transition:width .6s}
.fstage .fv{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:12.5px;font-weight:700;text-align:right}
.fstage .fbar:hover>span{filter:brightness(1.15)}

/* ============================================================ AGEING HEATMAP */
.heat{width:100%;border-collapse:separate;border-spacing:4px}
.heat th{background:none;border:none;position:static;text-transform:none;letter-spacing:0;color:var(--ink-3);
  font-size:11px;padding:4px;text-align:center;cursor:default}
.heat td:first-child{text-align:left;color:var(--ink-2);font-weight:600;font-size:12px;padding-left:2px}
.heat .cell{text-align:center;border-radius:7px;font-family:var(--mono);font-weight:700;font-size:13px;
  padding:11px 4px;color:#fff;cursor:pointer;transition:.12s;border:1px solid transparent}
.heat .cell:hover{transform:scale(1.05);border-color:rgba(255,255,255,.25)}
.heat .cell.z{color:var(--ink-3);background:var(--panel-2)!important}

/* ============================================================ TIMELINE / WEEK BARS */
.wkchart{display:flex;align-items:flex-end;gap:8px;height:180px;padding-top:10px}
.wkcol{flex:1;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:5px;height:100%;justify-content:flex-end;cursor:pointer}
.wkcol .stackv{width:100%;display:flex;flex-direction:column-reverse;border-radius:5px 5px 0 0;overflow:hidden;
  min-height:2px;transition:.2s}
.wkcol:hover .stackv{filter:brightness(1.12)}
.wkcol .stackv>span{transition:height .5s}
.wkcol .wl{font-size:10px;color:var(--ink-3);font-weight:600;font-family:var(--mono)}

/* ============================================================ DRAWER (drill-down) */
.scrim{position:fixed;inset:0;background:rgba(4,6,10,.62);backdrop-filter:blur(2px);z-index:80;opacity:0;
  pointer-events:none;transition:.2s}
.scrim.open{opacity:1;pointer-events:auto}
.drawer{position:fixed;top:0;right:0;bottom:0;width:min(560px,94vw);background:var(--bg-2);border-left:1px solid var(--line-2);
  z-index:90;transform:translateX(100%);transition:transform .28s cubic-bezier(.2,.8,.2,1);display:flex;flex-direction:column;
  box-shadow:-20px 0 50px -20px rgba(0,0,0,.7)}
.drawer.open{transform:none}
.drawer-h{padding:18px 22px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);display:flex;align-items:flex-start;gap:12px}
.drawer-h .x{margin-left:auto;background:var(--panel);border:1px solid var(--line);color:var(--ink-2);width:30px;height:30px;
  border-radius:8px;display:grid;place-items:center;flex-shrink:0}
.drawer-h .x:hover{background:var(--elev);color:var(--ink)}
.drawer-h h3{margin:0;font-size:16px;font-weight:700;line-height:1.3}
.drawer-h .code{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:12px;color:var(--brand);font-weight:600}
.drawer-b{flex:1;overflow-y:auto;padding:20px 22px}
.dfield{margin-bottom:16px}
.dfield .dl{font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.8px;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);font-weight:700;margin-bottom:5px}
.dfield .dv{font-size:13px;color:var(--ink);white-space:pre-wrap;line-height:1.55}
.dgrid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:13px;margin-bottom:16px}
.drow{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:8px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);font-size:12.5px}
.drow .dl2{color:var(--ink-3)} .drow .dv2{color:var(--ink);font-weight:600;text-align:right}
.dsection{font-size:11px;letter-spacing:1px;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--brand);font-weight:700;
  margin:20px 0 10px;padding-bottom:6px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
.linkrow{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:9px 11px;background:var(--panel);border:1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius:9px;margin-bottom:7px;cursor:pointer;transition:.12s}
.linkrow:hover{background:var(--elev);border-color:var(--line-2)}
.linkrow .code{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:12px;color:var(--brand);font-weight:600;flex-shrink:0}
.linkrow .lt{font-size:12px;color:var(--ink-2);overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;flex:1}

/* insight tiles */
.insight{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;padding:13px 15px;background:var(--panel);border:1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius:11px;cursor:pointer;transition:.15s}
.insight:hover{border-color:var(--line-2);background:var(--panel-2)}
.insight .ic{width:38px;height:38px;border-radius:10px;display:grid;place-items:center;flex-shrink:0}
.insight .ic svg{width:19px;height:19px}
.insight .n{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:22px;font-weight:700;line-height:1}
.insight .t{font-size:11.5px;color:var(--ink-2);font-weight:600;margin-top:2px}

/* toast */
.toast{position:fixed;bottom:22px;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%) translateY(20px);background:var(--panel-2);
  border:1px solid var(--line-2);color:var(--ink);padding:11px 18px;border-radius:10px;font-size:13px;font-weight:600;
  box-shadow:var(--shadow);z-index:200;opacity:0;transition:.25s;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:9px}
.toast.show{opacity:1;transform:translateX(-50%) translateY(0)}
.toast svg{width:16px;height:16px;color:var(--brand)}

.spark{display:flex;align-items:flex-end;gap:2px;height:26px}
.spark>span{flex:1;background:var(--brand-dim);border-radius:2px 2px 0 0;min-height:2px;opacity:.65}
.loader{position:fixed;inset:0;background:var(--bg);display:grid;place-items:center;z-index:500}
.loader .sp{width:38px;height:38px;border:3px solid var(--line);border-top-color:var(--brand);border-radius:50%;
  animation:spin .8s linear infinite}
@keyframes spin{to{transform:rotate(360deg)}}
.hidden{display:none!important}

@media print{
  .rail,.topbar,.filterbar,.card-tools,.scrim,.drawer,.top-actions{display:none!important}
  #app{display:block;height:auto}.main{overflow:visible;padding:0}
  body{overflow:visible;background:#fff;color:#000}
  .card,.kpi{break-inside:avoid;border-color:#ccc}
}
@media(max-width:900px){
  :root{--rail:0px}
  .rail{position:fixed;left:0;top:0;bottom:0;width:236px;z-index:100;transform:translateX(-100%);transition:.25s}
  .rail.open{transform:none;box-shadow:var(--shadow)}
  #app{grid-template-columns:1fr}
  .g-2,.g-3,.g-4{grid-template-columns:1fr}.span-2,.span-3{grid-column:span 1}
  .menu-btn{display:grid!important}
}
.menu-btn{display:none;place-items:center;width:34px;height:34px;background:var(--panel);border:1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius:8px;color:var(--ink)}
</style>
</head>
<body>
<div class="loader" id="loader"><div class="sp"></div></div>

<div id="app">
  <!-- ================= RAIL ================= -->
  <aside class="rail" id="rail">
    <div class="brand">
      <div class="logo">S</div>
      <div><h1>SDLC Command</h1><span>Delivery Control</span></div>
    </div>
    <nav class="nav" id="nav"></nav>
    <div class="rail-foot" id="railFoot">—</div>
  </aside>

  <!-- ================= TOPBAR ================= -->
  <header class="topbar">
    <button class="menu-btn" onclick="document.getElementById('rail').classList.toggle('open')">
      <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" width="18"><path d="M3 6h18M3 12h18M3 18h18"/></svg>
    </button>
    <div class="crumb" id="crumb">Executive</div>
    <div class="search">
      <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="7"/><path d="m21 21-4.3-4.3"/></svg>
      <input id="globalSearch" placeholder="Search requirements, stories, defects…  (/)" autocomplete="off">
      <div class="search-results" id="searchResults"></div>
    </div>
    <div class="top-actions">
      <input type="file" id="fileInput" accept=".xlsx,.xls,.csv" class="hidden">
      <button class="tbtn" onclick="document.getElementById('fileInput').click()" title="Reload data from Excel">
        <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path d="M21 12a9 9 0 1 1-2.6-6.4M21 3v6h-6"/></svg>Load Excel</button>
      <button class="tbtn" onclick="openLinkSettings()" title="Configure record hyperlinks">
        <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.5.6l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7-7l-1.7 1.7"/><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.5-.6l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7 7l1.7-1.7"/></svg>Links</button>
      <button class="tbtn" onclick="exportMenu(this)">
        <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path d="M12 3v12m0 0 4-4m-4 4-4-4M4 21h16"/></svg>Export</button>
      <div class="asof"><span>Data as of</span><b id="asOfLabel">—</b></div>
    </div>
  </header>

  <!-- ================= MAIN ================= -->
  <main class="main" id="main"></main>
</div>

<!-- drill-down drawer -->
<div class="scrim" id="scrim" onclick="closeDrawer()"></div>
<aside class="drawer" id="drawer"></aside>
<div class="toast" id="toast"></div>

Since the data is stored across multiple milestone fields (MS1–MS6), the query becomes very large and difficult to maintain. Additionally, Zoho Analytics does not support advanced SQL features required for dynamic calculations and data transformation. Because of these limitations, the current requirement cannot be implemented accurately using Query Table SQL alone. An alternative approach, such as data preprocessing or custom logic outside Zoho Analytics, will be required to achieve the expected result.", "ownerId": "2598652000187806720", "owner": "Padmapriya G", "requestedBy": "1598652000093285120", "requirement": "The business requires a milestone-based invoice tracking solution to monitor MS1–MS6 payment schedules, due dates, amounts received, outstanding balances, overdue invoices, and collection progress. "1598652000008469504", "requirement": "Implement an integrated task management framework between Zoho CRM and Zoho To Do, where tasks created under Open Activities within an Account are automatically synchronized to Zoho To Do.\nEnable assignment of tasks to multiple task owners, including users who do not have access to Zoho CRM.\nEnsure tasks are visible and manageable by both CRM and non-CRM users through Zoho To Do.\nProvide task visibility and tracking at the Account level, allowing users to view all associated tasks across customer accounts.\nSupport task status tracking with predefined statuses such as Open, Soon to be Closed, and Overdue.\nIntroduce mandatory Reason for Delay capture when task due dates are exceeded.\nAllow users to enter a Revised Due Date for overdue tasks while maintaining history of the original due date.\nConfigure automated notifications and reminders for task owners based on due dates and task status.\nImplement escalation workflows for overdue tasks to designated managers or stakeholders.\nEnable assignment of tasks to technical teams and cross-functional departments.\nProvide task owner-wise filtering, monitoring, and reporting capabilities.\nMake all task-related data available within Zoho Analytics for operational and management reporting.", "businessValue": "Better task governance, improved SLA compliance, enhanced team Revised Due Date, Escalation Status, and Task Category.\nImplement automated reminders before due dates and escalation notifications after overdue thresholds are reached.\nEnable account-level task visibility through related lists and linked task records within CRM.\nConfigure filters and views based on task owner, status, account, due date, and escalation level.\nMaintain audit history for due date revisions and delay reasons to improve accountability.\nIntegrate task data into Zoho Analytics to provide dashboards for task completion rates, overdue analysis, owner performance, escalation trends, and workload distribution.\nEnsure seamless collaboration between CRM and non-CRM users through centralized task visibility and tracking.", "userBenefits": "Multi-user task assignment, non-CRM reps can create proposals in the client’s preferred local currency while maintaining a consistent base currency (e.g., INR or USD) for HQ reporting.\n​\n\nAuto-fetch latest FX rates via API (e.g., OpenExchangeRates, XE, or Zoho Finance) and update conversion tables regularly to avoid manual entry and rate errors.\n​\n​\n\nDefine base currencies (INR, USD, EUR, etc.) and allow quote line items to be entered in local currencies, with an additional “Converted Value” field on Quote header and line items using real-time exchange rates.\n​\n\nEnsure proposal templates merge both local currency values and converted base currency values in PDFs sent to clients, while internal dashboards aggregate quote values across regions into a single reporting currency.\n​\n\nLock the conversion rate at proposal generation date (or approval date) to prevent fluctuations from changing approved quote amounts and provide an optional setting for territory-based default currencies for users.", "businessValue": "Improves financial accuracy and compliance, supports global pricing strategy, saves time for sales and finance teams, and enables reliable consolidated revenue forecasting across all geographies.", "solution": "Configure multi-currency settings in Zoho CRM/Finance by enabling multiple currencies, setting a corporate base currency, and creating territory-wise default currencies CRM currency records on a scheduled basis.\n​\n​\n\nAdd calculated fields on the Quote (e.g., “Base Currency Amount”) that multiply local currency totals by the applicable FX rate from the table, with logic to snapshot and store the rate at the time the Quote is created or moved to “Proposal Sent”.\n​\n\nUpdate quote/proposal templates to display both local and base currency amounts, and modify reporting/analytics to sum and visualize deal/quote values in the base currency using the stored snapshot rate rather than current rates.\n​\n\nImplement validation rules and user prompts to prevent saving a Quote if the FX rate is missing or outdated beyond a defined threshold, and add an admin tool/view to review and correct FX rates when needed.", "userBenefits": "Accurate and transparent pricing in client currency, reduced manual FX calculations, fewer quote errors, and easier comparison of proposals across regions.", "duration": "Short", "createdTime": "2025-07-17", "modifiedTime": "acceptance": "Each GEM Tender Deal includes a mandatory qualification section capturing at least: eligibility (technical/commercial), category fit (Design/Autodesk/AutoCAD), scope fit, price band, strategic fit, and risk indicators.\nWorkflow routes new GEM Tender Deals to designated reviewers based on geography (state/zone), product category, and tender value and assigns an owner once qualification is marked “Go”.\nSLA timers track:\nTime from tender import to first qualification decision.\nTime from qualification approval to proposal owner assignment.\nOverdue items trigger email/CRM notifications to the owner and manager, following an escalation style similar to the IndiaMart escalation pattern.\nChanges to key fields (qualification outcome, internal status, owner) are recorded in the history/timeline for audit and later reporting.\nQualification questions and internal status definitions are aligned with the steps shown in the GEM process video (search, eligibility "devById": null, "qaOwnerId": null, "buildStart": null, "buildComplete": null, "qaComplete": null, "uatComplete": null, "demoDate": null, "deployDate": null, "estEndDate": null, "estEndAging": null, "completion": 50, "weekNo": null, "wkStart": null, "acceptance": "SPMS-US-001 – Quarterly Record Creation\n\nButton creates next quarter SPMS records for all users.\n\nTarget fields initialize empty.\n\nAdmin can manually update targets.\n\nSPMS-US-002 – Daily Job Execution\n\nRuns daily at 7 PM.\n\nRecalculates Actuals and Deal Count.\n\nUpdates Deal–SPMS links and pushes to Analytics.\n\nSPMS-US-003 – Weekly Job Execution\n\nRuns every Saturday 7 PM.\n\nRolls forward Backlogs.\n\nSyncs weekly summary to Analytics.\n\nSPMS-US-004 – Deal Linking\n\nDeals auto-link to correct SPMS record by Owner, Stage, Week.\n\nPrevents duplicates.\n\nSPMS-US-005 – Custom Views\n\nSales Executive, Team Lead, and Org views available.\n\nRole-based visibility.\n\nSPMS-US-006 – Target Calculations\n\nTarget incl. Backlog = Target + Backlog.\n\nAuto-updates when fields change.\n\nSPMS-US-007 – Analytics Sync\n\nData auto-pushes after schedulers run.\n\nSSD and Krion 6D dashboards refresh daily.\n\nSPMS-US-008 – Access Control\n\nSales Exec: own data only.\n\nTeam Lead: team data.\n\nAdmin: full access.\n\nSPMS-US-009 – Backlog Carry Forward\n\nUnachieved targets auto-roll to next week’s Backlog.\n\nSPMS-US-010 – Dashboard Display\n\nDashboards show Target, Actual, Backlog, Deal Count.\n\nUpdates daily from SPMS data.", "description": "SPMS – Deluge Solution Summary\n\nCustom Module: SPMS_Records (tracks weekly targets & actuals).\n\nFields: Target, Backlog, Target+Backlog, Actual, Deal Count, Linked Deals.\n\nButtons: Create Next Quarter, Sales Exec View, Team Lead View, Org View.\n\nDeluge Jobs:\n\ncreateQuarterSPMSRecords() → create blank targets.\n\ndailySPMSUpdate() → update Actuals & Deals (7 PM daily).\n\nweeklySPMSUpdate() → roll Backlog + sync Analytics (Sat 7 PM).\n\nIntegrations: Deals auto-link to SPMS via multi-lookup; data pushed to Zoho Analytics.\n\nAccess: Role-based (Exec / Lead / Admin).", "techNotes": null, "createdTime": "2025-10-07", "modifiedTime": "2026-07-24", "lastActivity": "2026-07-24"}, {"id": "2598652000171729408", "code": "U-2017", "subject": "Aging Report in Zoho SheetAn Aging Report should be maintained automatically in Zoho Sheet.It should include the following columns:", "reqFk": "1598652000167846912", "parentFk": null, "totalPlanned": null, "totalActual": null, "weekPlanned": null, "weekActual": null, "pointsEst": 1, "pointsCons": 2, "pointsRem": 0, "stage": "Deployed", "status": "In Progress", "tdd": null, "ownerId": "2598652000053812736", "owner": "Nithish R", "devById": "1598652000150231040", "qaOwnerId": "2598652000053812736", "buildStart": "2025-11-21", "buildComplete": null, "qaComplete": null, "uatComplete": null, "demoDate": null, "deployDate": null, "estEndDate": null, "estEndAging": null, "completion": 100, "weekNo": null, "wkStart": null, "acceptance": "If the escalation criteria workflow is done & integrated with Zoho sheet", "description": "4. IndiaMart to CRM", "fixSize": 1, "dependency": null, "reportedDate": "2026-07-16", "expectedDate": "2026-07-16", "revisedDate": null, "completion": null, "createdTime": "2026-07-21", "modifiedTime": "2026-07-30", "lastActivity": "2026-07-30"}, {"id": "1598652000180719104", "code": "D-4003", "reqFk": null, "storyFk": null, "status": "Fix Deployed", "severity": "High", "category": "Solution", "environment": "Prod", "ownerId": "2598652000053812736", "owner": "Nithish R", "reportedById": null, "fixedById": null, "qaOwnerId": null, "description": "Account attributes not updated on Krion6D account layout", "rootCause": null, "fixDetails": null, "fixSize": null, "dependency": null, "reportedDate": "2026-05-05", "expectedDate": "2026-05-07", "revisedDate": null, "completion": null, "createdTime": "2025-12-26", "modifiedTime": "2026-07-30", "lastActivity": "2026-07-30"}, {"id": "2598652000196588544", "code": "D-4010", "reqFk": "1598652000182373120", "storyFk": "1598652000181765632", "status": "Fix Deployed", "severity": "Medium", "category": "Solution", "environment": "Prod", "ownerId": "1598652000150231040", "owner": "Sanjaybabu C", "reportedById": "2598652000053812736", "fixedById": "1598652000150231040", "qaOwnerId": "1598652000095172096", "description": "API Issue in Sales Order Creation", "rootCause": null, "fixDetails": "Product Line API name Issue, now change the Upper case to Lower Case.", "fixSize": 2, "dependency": "Some APIs Issue. In backend API Name Issue, Need Check with Zoho Support Team.", "reportedDate": "2026-05-06", "expectedDate": "2026-04-01", "revisedDate": null, "completion": null, "createdTime": "2026-04-10", "modifiedTime": "2026-07-30", "lastActivity": "2026-07-30"}]}</script>
<script>
/* =====================================================================================
   SDLC ENTERPRISE DASHBOARD  —  application script
   Architecture:
     1. CONFIG          semantic maps, palette, thresholds
     2. UTIL            date/format/dom/csv helpers
     3. DATA LAYER      ingest + normalize + relationship mapping   (Store)
     4. ENGINE          KPI / completion / ageing / delay / risk / health calculators
     5. FILTERS         global filter + search state
     6. RENDER          per-view renderers + shared components
     7. DRILL-DOWN      drawer detail views
     8. EXPORT          CSV / Excel / PDF
     9. ROUTER + INIT
   ===================================================================================== */

/* =====================================================================================
   1) CONFIG — semantic maps, palette, thresholds
   ===================================================================================== */
const CFG = {
  color:{ ok:'#39d98a', warn:'#f0b429', danger:'#ff5c6c', info:'#5aa9ff', violet:'#9d8bff',
    brand:'#2de1c2', orange:'#ff924d', pink:'#ff6fb5', blue:'#5aa9ff', neutral:'#6b7889',
    grid:'#28313f', ink2:'#a3b1c4', ink3:'#6b7889' },
  // Requirement statuses considered "done"
  reqDone:['Complete - In Prod'],
  reqParked:['Deferred','Hold','Dup'],
  reqBacklog:['Accepted - Backlog','Created','Need More Info','Grooming'],
  // Story pipeline order (index used to derive % when Completion missing)
  stageOrder:['Backlog','Definition','Design','Build','Unit Testing','QA','UAT','Prod Validation','Deployed','Blocked','NOT Needed'],
  storyDone:['Complete'],
  storyDoneStage:['Deployed'],
  storyBlocked:['Blocked','Waiting - Dependency'],
  // Defect resolution
  defResolved:['Fix Deployed','Fixed',"Can't Reproduce"],
  // ageing buckets (days)
  ageBuckets:[{k:'0-7',lo:0,hi:7},{k:'8-14',lo:8,hi:14},{k:'15-30',lo:15,hi:30},
    {k:'31-60',lo:31,hi:60},{k:'60+',lo:61,hi:1e9}],
  // risk weights
  risk:{ overdue:34, criticalDefect:22, blocked:16, priority:14, staleness:14 }
};
const STAGE_COLOR = { 'Backlog':'#6b7889','Definition':'#7d8aa0','Design':'#9d8bff','Build':'#5aa9ff',
  'Unit Testing':'#4dc9d6','QA':'#f0b429','UAT':'#ff924d','Prod Validation':'#2de1c2','Deployed':'#39d98a',
  'Blocked':'#ff5c6c','NOT Needed':'#4a5568' };
const PRIORITY_RANK = { 'Critical':4,'High':3,'Medium':2,'Low':1 };

/* ---- external record hyperlinks (configurable URL pattern) ---- */
const Ext={
  tpl:{req:'',story:'',defect:''},
  load(){ try{ const s=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('sdlc_link_templates')); if(s)this.tpl={...this.tpl,...s}; }catch(e){} },
  save(){ try{ localStorage.setItem('sdlc_link_templates',JSON.stringify(this.tpl)); }catch(e){} },
  url(kind,id,code){ const t=this.tpl[kind]; if(!t||!id)return null;
    return t.replace(/\{id\}/g,encodeURIComponent(id)).replace(/\{code\}/g,encodeURIComponent(code||'')); },
  has(){ return !!(this.tpl.req||this.tpl.story||this.tpl.defect); }
};
// primary code cell → hyperlink if a URL pattern is configured, else plain code (row still opens drawer)
function codeLink(kind,id,code,color){ color=color||'var(--brand)';
  const u=Ext.url(kind,id,code);
  if(u) return `<a class="code extlink" style="color:${color}" href="${esc(u)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" onclick="event.stopPropagation()" title="Open ${esc(code)} in source system">${esc(code)} ↗</a>`;
  return `<span class="code" style="color:${color}">${esc(code)}</span>`;
}
function idForCode(kind,code){ const e = kind==='req'?Store.req.find(r=>r.code===code)
  : kind==='story'?Store.stories.find(s=>s.code===code) : Store.defects.find(d=>d.code===code); return e?e.id:null; }
function fkCodeLink(kind,code,color){ if(!code)return '—'; return codeLink(kind,idForCode(kind,code),code,color); }
// requirement code → opens the internal User-Story tracking screen for that requirement
function reqTrackLink(id,code,color){ color=color||'var(--brand)'; if(!code)return '—';
  return `<a class="code extlink" style="color:${color}" onclick="event.stopPropagation();goReqTrack('${esc(code)}')" title="Open ${esc(code)} — user story tracking">${esc(code)} ↗</a>`; }
function reqTrackLinkByCode(code,color){ if(!code)return '—'; return reqTrackLink(idForCode('req',code),code,color); }

/* =====================================================================================
   2) UTIL — helpers
   ===================================================================================== */
const $ = (s,r=document)=>r.querySelector(s);
const $$ = (s,r=document)=>[...r.querySelectorAll(s)];
const el = (t,c,h)=>{const e=document.createElement(t); if(c)e.className=c; if(h!=null)e.innerHTML=h; return e;};
const num = v => (typeof v==='number'&&!isNaN(v))?v:null;
const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v));
const esc = s => (s==null?'':String(s)).replace(/[&<>"]/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c]));
const keyOf = v => v==null?null:(typeof v==='number'?v.toFixed(0):String(v).trim());
const DAY = 86400000;
function pd(s){ if(!s)return null; if(s instanceof Date)return s; const d=new Date(s); return isNaN(d)?null:d; }
function daysBetween(a,b){ a=pd(a); b=pd(b); if(!a||!b)return null; return Math.round((b-a)/DAY); }
function fmtDate(s){ const d=pd(s); if(!d)return '—';
  return d.toLocaleDateString('en-US',{day:'2-digit',month:'short',year:'numeric'}); }
function fmtShort(s){ const d=pd(s); if(!d)return '—';
  return d.toLocaleDateString('en-US',{day:'2-digit',month:'short'}); }
const pct = v => v==null?'—':Math.round(v)+'%';
const nz = (v,d='—') => (v==null||v==='')?d:v;
function debounce(fn,ms){let t;return(...a)=>{clearTimeout(t);t=setTimeout(()=>fn(...a),ms);};}
function toast(msg){ const t=$('#toast'); t.innerHTML='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"/></svg>'+esc(msg);
  t.classList.add('show'); clearTimeout(toast._t); toast._t=setTimeout(()=>t.classList.remove('show'),2600); }
function ico(p){ return '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.9" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">'+p+'</svg>'; }
const ICONS={
  exec:'<path d="M3 3v18h18"/><rect x="7" y="10" width="3" height="7"/><rect x="12" y="6" width="3" height="11"/><rect x="17" y="13" width="3" height="4"/>',
  req:'<path d="M9 5H7a2 2 0 0 0-2 2v12a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2V7a2 2 0 0 0-2-2h-2"/><rect x="9" y="3" width="6" height="4" rx="1"/><path d="M9 12h6M9 16h4"/>',
  story:'<path d="M4 19.5A2.5 2.5 0 0 1 6.5 17H20"/><path d="M6.5 2H20v20H6.5A2.5 2.5 0 0 1 4 19.5v-15A2.5 2.5 0 0 1 6.5 2z"/>',
  defect:'<path d="m8 2 1.9 1.9M16 2l-1.9 1.9M12 20v-8"/><rect x="8" y="6" width="8" height="12" rx="4"/><path d="M4 13H2m20 0h-2M5 8 3 7m18 1 2-1M5 18l-2 1m18-1 2 1"/>',
  week:'<rect x="3" y="4" width="18" height="18" rx="2"/><path d="M16 2v4M8 2v4M3 10h18"/>',
  proj:'<path d="M2 20h20M4 20V10l4-3 4 3 4-2 4 3v9"/>',
  dev:'<path d="m16 18 6-6-6-6M8 6l-6 6 6 6"/>',
  age:'<circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"/><path d="M12 7v5l3 2"/>',
  risk:'<path d="M10.3 3.3 1.8 18a2 2 0 0 0 1.7 3h17a2 2 0 0 0 1.7-3L13.7 3.3a2 2 0 0 0-3.4 0z"/><path d="M12 9v4M12 17h.01"/>',
  check:'<path d="M22 11.1V12a10 10 0 1 1-5.9-9.1"/><path d="m9 11 3 3L22 4"/>',
  alert:'<circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"/><path d="M12 8v4M12 16h.01"/>',
  clock:'<circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"/><path d="M12 7v5l3 2"/>',
  fire:'<path d="M12 2c1 4 4 5 4 9a4 4 0 0 1-8 0c0-1 .5-2 1-3 .5 2 2 2 2 2s-1-3 1-8z"/>',
  block:'<circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"/><path d="m5 5 14 14"/>',
  up:'<path d="M7 17 17 7M7 7h10v10"/>',
  point:'<circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"/><path d="M12 8v8M8 12h8"/>',
  deploy:'<path d="M12 2 2 7l10 5 10-5-10-5zM2 17l10 5 10-5M2 12l10 5 10-5"/>',
  people:'<path d="M17 21v-2a4 4 0 0 0-4-4H5a4 4 0 0 0-4 4v2"/><circle cx="9" cy="7" r="4"/><path d="M23 21v-2a4 4 0 0 0-3-3.9M16 3.1a4 4 0 0 1 0 7.8"/>',
  link:'<path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.5.6l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7-7l-1.7 1.7"/><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.5-.6l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7 7l1.7-1.7"/>'
};

/* =====================================================================================
   3) DATA LAYER — ingest, normalize, relationship mapping (single pipeline)
   ===================================================================================== */
const Store = {
  raw:{requirements:[],stories:[],defects:[],people:{}},
  req:[], stories:[], defects:[],
  byReqId:new Map(), byStoryId:new Map(),
  people:{}, asOf:null,
  modules:[], owners:[], weeks:[],

  ingest(raw){
    this.raw = raw;
    this.people = raw.people||{};
    const nameOf = id => this.people[keyOf(id)] || (id?('User '+keyOf(id).slice(-4)):'—');

    // asOf = latest activity timestamp in the data (data-refresh reference)
    let latest=0;
    const bump=d=>{const t=pd(d); if(t&&t>latest)latest=+t;};
    (raw.requirements||[]).forEach(r=>{bump(r.lastActivity);bump(r.modifiedTime);});
    (raw.stories||[]).forEach(r=>{bump(r.lastActivity);bump(r.modifiedTime);bump(r.deployDate);});
    (raw.defects||[]).forEach(r=>{bump(r.lastActivity);bump(r.modifiedTime);});
    this.asOf = latest?new Date(latest):new Date();
    const AS=this.asOf;

    /* ---- Requirements ---- */
    this.req = (raw.requirements||[]).map(r=>{
      const done = CFG.reqDone.includes(r.status) || r.completion>=100;
      const parked = CFG.reqParked.includes(r.status);
      const backlog = CFG.reqBacklog.includes(r.status);
      const due = r.revisedDate||r.expectedDate;
      const overdue = (!done&&!parked&&due&&pd(due)<AS)?daysBetween(due,AS):0;
      const remaining = (!done&&due&&pd(due)>=AS)?daysBetween(AS,due):null;
      const comp = num(r.completion)!=null?r.completion:(done?100:0);
      const age = daysBetween(r.startDate||r.createdTime, done?(r.modifiedTime||AS):AS);
      return {...r, _kind:'req', owner:r.owner||nameOf(r.ownerId),
        done, parked, backlog, inProgress:!done&&!parked&&!backlog,
        due, overdue:overdue||0, isOverdue:overdue>0, remaining, comp, age:age||0,
        stories:[], defects:[] };
    });
    this.byReqId = new Map(this.req.map(r=>[keyOf(r.id),r])); // note: numeric ids may collide (source precision)
    this.byReqCode = new Map(this.req.map(r=>[r.code,r]));
    this.reqByKey = new Map(); this.req.forEach(r=>{const k=keyOf(r.id); if(!this.reqByKey.has(k))this.reqByKey.set(k,[]); this.reqByKey.get(k).push(r);});
    this.req.forEach(r=>{ const g=this.reqByKey.get(keyOf(r.id)); r.idCollision=g.length>1; r.collisionCodes=g.filter(x=>x!==r).map(x=>x.code); });

    /* ---- Stories ---- */
    this.stories = (raw.stories||[]).map(s=>{
      const done = CFG.storyDone.includes(s.status) || CFG.storyDoneStage.includes(s.stage) || s.completion>=100;
      const blocked = CFG.storyBlocked.includes(s.status) || s.stage==='Blocked';
      const notStarted = (s.status==='NOT Started') || (!done&&!blocked&&(s.completion==null||s.completion===0)&&!s.buildStart);
      const stageIdx = CFG.stageOrder.indexOf(s.stage);
      let comp = num(s.completion);
      if(comp==null) comp = done?100:(stageIdx>=0?Math.round(stageIdx/(CFG.stageOrder.length-1)*100):0);
      const est=0, cons=0, rem=0; // points deprecated — hours-only
      const totalPlanned=num(s.totalPlanned)||null, totalActual=num(s.totalActual)||null;
      const weekPlanned=num(s.weekPlanned)||null, weekActual=num(s.weekActual)||null;
      const due = s.revisedDate||s.estEndDate;
      const overdue = (!done&&!blocked&&due&&pd(due)<AS)?daysBetween(due,AS):0;
      const remaining = (!done&&due&&pd(due)>=AS)?daysBetween(AS,due):null;
      const pendingQA = !done&&!blocked&&(s.stage==='QA'||(s.buildComplete&&!s.qaComplete&&['Build','Unit Testing','QA'].includes(s.stage)));
      const pendingDeploy = !done&&(['UAT','Prod Validation'].includes(s.stage)||(s.uatComplete&&!s.deployDate));
      const cycle = daysBetween(s.buildStart||s.createdTime, done?(s.deployDate||s.modifiedTime||AS):AS);
      return {...s, _kind:'story', owner:s.owner||nameOf(s.ownerId),
        devBy:nameOf(s.devById), qaOwner:nameOf(s.qaOwnerId),
        done, blocked, notStarted, inProgress:!done&&!blocked&&!notStarted,
        totalPlanned, totalActual, weekPlanned, weekActual,
        stageIdx, comp, est, cons, rem, due, overdue:overdue||0, isOverdue:overdue>0,
        remaining, pendingQA, pendingDeploy, age:cycle||0, reqCode:null };
    });
    this.byStoryId = new Map(this.stories.map(s=>[keyOf(s.id),s]));
    this.byStoryCode = new Map(this.stories.map(s=>[s.code,s]));
    this.storyByKey = new Map(); this.stories.forEach(s=>{const k=keyOf(s.id); if(!this.storyByKey.has(k))this.storyByKey.set(k,[]); this.storyByKey.get(k).push(s);});

    /* ---- Defects ---- */
    this.defects = (raw.defects||[]).map(d=>{
      const resolved = CFG.defResolved.includes(d.status);
      const open = !resolved;
      const critical = ['High','Critical'].includes(d.severity);
      const age = daysBetween(d.reportedDate||d.createdTime, resolved?(d.modifiedTime||AS):AS);
      return {...d, _kind:'defect', owner:d.owner||nameOf(d.ownerId),
        fixedBy:nameOf(d.fixedById),
        resolved, open, critical, openCritical:open&&critical, age:age||0,
        reqCode:null, storyCode:null };
    });

    /* ---- Relationship mapping (collision-aware: FKs are numeric & may collide, so attach to every candidate) ---- */
    this.storyByReq = new Map();
    this.stories.forEach(s=>{
      const cands=this.reqByKey.get(keyOf(s.reqFk))||[];
      s.reqAmbiguous=cands.length>1; s.reqCandidates=cands.map(r=>r.code);
      cands.forEach(r=>r.stories.push(s));
      if(cands[0]){ s.reqCode=cands[0].code; s.reqSubject=cands[0].subject; s.moduleName=cands[0].module; s.reqOwner=cands[0].owner; }
    });
    this.defects.forEach(d=>{
      const rc=this.reqByKey.get(keyOf(d.reqFk))||[];
      rc.forEach(r=>r.defects.push(d));
      if(rc[0]){ d.reqCode=rc[0].code; d.moduleName=rc[0].module; d.reqAmbiguous=rc.length>1; }
      const sc=this.storyByKey.get(keyOf(d.storyFk))||[];
      if(sc[0]){ d.storyCode=sc[0].code; if(!d.reqCode&&sc[0].reqCode){d.reqCode=sc[0].reqCode; d.moduleName=sc[0].moduleName;} }
    });
    this.stories.forEach(s=>{ const p=this.storyByKey.get(keyOf(s.parentFk))||[]; s.parent=p[0]||null;
      s.children=this.stories.filter(x=>keyOf(x.parentFk)===keyOf(s.id)); });

    /* ---- Dimensions for filters ---- */
    this.modules = [...new Set(this.req.map(r=>r.module).filter(Boolean))].sort();
    this.owners = [...new Set([...this.req.map(r=>r.owner),...this.stories.map(s=>s.owner),
      ...this.defects.map(d=>d.owner)].filter(Boolean))].sort();
    this.weeks = [...new Set(this.stories.map(s=>s.weekNo).filter(v=>v!=null))].sort((a,b)=>a-b);
    return this;
  },

  // find any entity by code (search + deep-link)
  findByCode(code){
    code=(code||'').toUpperCase();
    return this.req.find(r=>(r.code||'').toUpperCase()===code)
      || this.stories.find(s=>(s.code||'').toUpperCase()===code)
      || this.defects.find(d=>(d.code||'').toUpperCase()===code);
  }
};

/* =====================================================================================
   4) ENGINE — KPI / completion / ageing / delay / risk / health calculators
   All computed from the (optionally filtered) working set → dynamic on data change.
   ===================================================================================== */
const Engine = {
  reqStats(list){
    const t=list.length, done=list.filter(r=>r.done).length,
      prog=list.filter(r=>r.inProgress).length, back=list.filter(r=>r.backlog).length,
      parked=list.filter(r=>r.parked).length, over=list.filter(r=>r.isOverdue).length;
    const active=list.filter(r=>!r.parked);
    const comp = active.length? Math.round(active.reduce((a,r)=>a+(r.comp||0),0)/active.length):0;
    return {t,done,prog,back,parked,over,comp,
      pctDone:t?Math.round(done/t*100):0};
  },
  storyStats(list){
    const t=list.length, done=list.filter(s=>s.done).length,
      prog=list.filter(s=>s.inProgress).length, blocked=list.filter(s=>s.blocked).length,
      notStarted=list.filter(s=>s.notStarted).length, over=list.filter(s=>s.isOverdue).length,
      qa=list.filter(s=>s.pendingQA).length, dep=list.filter(s=>s.pendingDeploy).length;
    const totalP=list.reduce((a,s)=>a+(s.totalPlanned||0),0), totalA=list.reduce((a,s)=>a+(s.totalActual||0),0);
    const weekP=list.reduce((a,s)=>a+(s.weekPlanned||0),0), weekA=list.reduce((a,s)=>a+(s.weekActual||0),0);
    const withTP=list.filter(s=>s.totalPlanned!=null).length, withTA=list.filter(s=>s.totalActual!=null).length;
    const withWP=list.filter(s=>s.weekPlanned!=null).length, withWA=list.filter(s=>s.weekActual!=null).length;
    const comp=t?Math.round(list.reduce((a,s)=>a+(s.comp||0),0)/t):0;
    return {t,done,prog,blocked,notStarted,over,qa,dep,comp,
      totalP,totalA,weekP,weekA,withTP,withTA,withWP,withWA,
      hoursPct:totalP?Math.round(totalA/totalP*100):0,
      weekPct:weekP?Math.round(weekA/weekP*100):0,
      pctDone:t?Math.round(done/t*100):0};
  },
  defectStats(list){
    const t=list.length, open=list.filter(d=>d.open).length, resolved=list.filter(d=>d.resolved).length,
      crit=list.filter(d=>d.openCritical).length;
    return {t,open,resolved,crit, pctResolved:t?Math.round(resolved/t*100):0};
  },
  // overall SDLC completion = weighted blend of the three streams
  overall(){
    const r=this.reqStats(Store.req), s=this.storyStats(Store.stories), d=this.defectStats(Store.defects);
    const val = Math.round(r.comp*0.35 + s.comp*0.45 + d.pctResolved*0.20);
    return {val, req:r, story:s, defect:d};
  },
  // ageing distribution for a list using each item's .age, filtered to "open" ones
  ageing(list,openPred){
    const buckets=CFG.ageBuckets.map(b=>({...b,n:0,items:[]}));
    list.filter(openPred).forEach(x=>{
      const a=x.age||0; const b=buckets.find(b=>a>=b.lo&&a<=b.hi); if(b){b.n++;b.items.push(x);}
    });
    return buckets;
  },
  // per-requirement rollup ("project" progress)
  reqRollup(){
    return Store.req.map(r=>{
      const ss=this.storyStats(r.stories), ds=this.defectStats(r.defects);
      // blended completion: prefer requirement completion, blend with story progress if stories exist
      let comp=r.comp;
      if(r.stories.length) comp=Math.round(r.comp*0.5 + ss.comp*0.5);
      return {req:r, stories:ss, defects:ds, comp,
        health:this.healthScore(r,ss,ds), risk:this.riskScore(r,ss,ds)};
    });
  },
  // developer productivity (by developer / owner)
  productivity(){
    const map=new Map();
    const get=n=>{ if(!map.has(n))map.set(n,{name:n,owned:0,developed:0,done:0,pts:0,ptsDone:0,
      cycle:[],defectsFixed:0,blocked:0,inProg:0,open:0}); return map.get(n); };
    Store.stories.forEach(s=>{
      const o=get(s.owner); o.owned++; if(s.done){o.done++;o.ptsDone+=s.cons||0;} else o.open++;
      o.pts+=s.est||0; if(s.blocked)o.blocked++; if(s.inProgress)o.inProg++;
      if(s.done&&s.age) o.cycle.push(s.age);
      if(s.devBy&&s.devBy!=='—'){ const d=get(s.devBy); if(d!==o)d.developed++; else o.developed++; }
    });
    Store.defects.forEach(d=>{ if(d.fixedBy&&d.fixedBy!=='—') get(d.fixedBy).defectsFixed++; });
    return [...map.values()].map(p=>({...p,
      avgCycle:p.cycle.length?Math.round(p.cycle.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/p.cycle.length):null,
      pctDone:p.owned?Math.round(p.done/p.owned*100):0}))
      .filter(p=>p.name&&p.name!=='—')
      .sort((a,b)=>b.ptsDone-a.ptsDone);
  },
  // weekly delivery aggregation
  weekly(){
    const map=new Map();
    Store.stories.forEach(s=>{ if(s.weekNo==null)return;
      if(!map.has(s.weekNo))map.set(s.weekNo,{wk:s.weekNo,start:s.wkStart,planned:0,done:0,blocked:0,pts:0,ptsDone:0,deployed:0,items:[]});
      const w=map.get(s.weekNo); w.planned++; w.items.push(s); w.pts+=s.est||0;
      if(s.done){w.done++;w.ptsDone+=s.cons||0;} if(s.blocked)w.blocked++;
      if(s.deployDate)w.deployed++;
      if(!w.start&&s.wkStart)w.start=s.wkStart;
    });
    return [...map.values()].sort((a,b)=>a.wk-b.wk);
  },
  // risk score 0-100 (higher = riskier) with contributing factors
  riskScore(r,ss,ds){
    ss=ss||this.storyStats(r.stories); ds=ds||this.defectStats(r.defects);
    let s=0; const f=[];
    if(r.isOverdue){ const p=Math.min(CFG.risk.overdue, 10+r.overdue*0.6); s+=p; f.push(['Overdue '+r.overdue+'d',p]); }
    if(ds.crit){ const p=Math.min(CFG.risk.criticalDefect, ds.crit*11); s+=p; f.push([ds.crit+' open critical defect'+(ds.crit>1?'s':''),p]); }
    if(ss.blocked){ const p=Math.min(CFG.risk.blocked, ss.blocked*7); s+=p; f.push([ss.blocked+' blocked stor'+(ss.blocked>1?'ies':'y'),p]); }
    const pr=(PRIORITY_RANK[r.priority]||2); if(pr>=3&&!r.done){ const p=(pr-2)*7; s+=p; f.push([r.priority+' priority',p]); }
    // staleness: elapsed vs completion
    if(!r.done&&r.due){ const el=daysBetween(r.startDate||r.createdTime,Store.asOf), tot=daysBetween(r.startDate||r.createdTime,r.due);
      if(el&&tot&&tot>0){ const expected=clamp(el/tot*100,0,100); const gap=expected-(r.comp||0);
        if(gap>15){ const p=Math.min(CFG.risk.staleness, gap*0.25); s+=p; f.push(['Behind schedule ~'+Math.round(gap)+'%',p]); } } }
    return {score:Math.round(clamp(s,0,100)), factors:f.sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1])};
  },
  healthScore(r,ss,ds){
    const risk=this.riskScore(r,ss,ds).score;
    return Math.round(clamp(100-risk,0,100));
  },
  // portfolio-level health
  portfolioHealth(){
    const roll=this.reqRollup().filter(x=>!x.req.parked);
    if(!roll.length)return {score:100,band:'Healthy'};
    const avg=Math.round(roll.reduce((a,x)=>a+x.health,0)/roll.length);
    const band = avg>=75?'Healthy':avg>=50?'At Risk':'Critical';
    return {score:avg,band,atRisk:roll.filter(x=>x.health<50).length,total:roll.length};
  }
};

/* =====================================================================================
   5) FILTERS — global filter + search state
   ===================================================================================== */
const Filters = { module:'', owner:'', priority:'', status:'', storyStatus:'', q:'', dateFrom:'', dateTo:'', buildFrom:'', buildTo:'' };
// dates used by the delivery-date filter, per entity type (requirement matches EITHER expected or revised)
function filterDates(x,kind){
  if(kind==='req') return [x.expectedDate,x.revisedDate].filter(Boolean);
  if(kind==='story') return [x.estEndDate].filter(Boolean);
  return [x.expectedDate,x.reportedDate].filter(Boolean);
}
function applyFilters(list,kind){
  return list.filter(x=>{
    if(Filters.module){ const m = kind==='req'?x.module:(x.moduleName||''); if(m!==Filters.module) return false; }
    if(Filters.owner && x.owner!==Filters.owner) return false;
    if(Filters.priority && kind==='req' && x.priority!==Filters.priority) return false;
    if(Filters.priority && kind==='defect' && x.severity!==Filters.priority) return false;
    if(Filters.status && kind==='req' && x.status!==Filters.status) return false;
    if(Filters.storyStatus && kind==='story'){ const st=x.status||'(blank)'; if(st!==Filters.storyStatus) return false; }
    if(kind==='story' && (Filters.buildFrom||Filters.buildTo)){
      const d=x.buildStart; if(!d) return false;
      if(Filters.buildFrom && d < Filters.buildFrom) return false;
      if(Filters.buildTo && d > Filters.buildTo) return false;
    }
    if(kind==='defect' && (Filters.buildFrom||Filters.buildTo)){
      const d=x.expectedDate||x.createdTime; if(!d) return false;
      if(Filters.buildFrom && d < Filters.buildFrom) return false;
      if(Filters.buildTo && d > Filters.buildTo) return false;
    }
    if(Filters.dateFrom||Filters.dateTo){
      const ds=filterDates(x,kind);
      if(!ds.length) return false;
      const ok=ds.some(d=>(!Filters.dateFrom||d>=Filters.dateFrom)&&(!Filters.dateTo||d<=Filters.dateTo));
      if(!ok) return false;
    }
    if(Filters.q){ const q=Filters.q.toLowerCase();
      const hay=[x.code,x.subject,x.description,x.requirement,x.status,x.stage,x.owner,x.moduleName,x.module]
        .filter(Boolean).join(' ').toLowerCase();
      if(!hay.includes(q)) return false; }
    return true;
  });
}
// working sets (respect global filters) — recomputed on demand
const WS = {
  req:()=>applyFilters(Store.req,'req'),
  stories:()=>applyFilters(Store.stories,'story'),
  defects:()=>applyFilters(Store.defects,'defect')
};

function renderFilterBar(kinds){
  const opt=(v,cur)=>`<option value="${esc(v)}" ${v===cur?'selected':''}>${esc(v||'All')}</option>`;
  const modOpts=['',...Store.modules].map(v=>opt(v,Filters.module)).join('');
  const ownOpts=['',...Store.owners].map(v=>opt(v,Filters.owner)).join('');
  const priOpts=['','Critical','High','Medium','Low'].map(v=>opt(v,Filters.priority)).join('');
  const active = (Filters.module||Filters.owner||Filters.priority||Filters.status||Filters.q||Filters.dateFrom||Filters.dateTo||Filters.buildFrom||Filters.buildTo);
  const showDate = kinds && (kinds.includes('req')||kinds.includes('story'));
  const dateLbl = kinds && kinds.includes('story') && !kinds.includes('req') ? 'Est. End' : 'Delivery';
  return `<div class="filterbar">
    <div class="fsel"><label>Module</label><select onchange="Filters.module=this.value;Router.refresh()">${modOpts}</select></div>
    <div class="fsel"><label>Owner</label><select onchange="Filters.owner=this.value;Router.refresh()">${ownOpts}</select></div>
    <div class="fsel"><label>${kinds&&kinds.includes('defect')?'Severity':'Priority'}</label><select onchange="Filters.priority=this.value;Router.refresh()">${priOpts}</select></div>
    ${showDate?`<div class="fsel noarrow"><label>${dateLbl} From</label><input type="date" value="${esc(Filters.dateFrom)}" onchange="Filters.dateFrom=this.value;Router.refresh()"></div>
    <div class="fsel noarrow"><label>${dateLbl} To</label><input type="date" value="${esc(Filters.dateTo)}" onchange="Filters.dateTo=this.value;Router.refresh()"></div>`:''}
    ${kinds&&kinds.includes('story')?`<div class="fsel noarrow"><label>Build Start From</label><input type="date" value="${esc(Filters.buildFrom)}" onchange="Filters.buildFrom=this.value;Router.refresh()"></div>
    <div class="fsel noarrow"><label>Build Start To</label><input type="date" value="${esc(Filters.buildTo)}" onchange="Filters.buildTo=this.value;Router.refresh()"></div>`:''}
    ${kinds&&kinds.includes('defect')?`<div class="fsel noarrow"><label>Est End From</label><input type="date" value="${esc(Filters.buildFrom)}" onchange="Filters.buildFrom=this.value;Router.refresh()"></div>
    <div class="fsel noarrow"><label>Est End To</label><input type="date" value="${esc(Filters.buildTo)}" onchange="Filters.buildTo=this.value;Router.refresh()"></div>`:''}
    ${active?`<button class="clearf" onclick="clearFilters()">✕ Clear filters</button>`:''}
    <div class="chipcount" id="chipCount"></div>
  </div>`;
}
function clearFilters(){ Filters.module=Filters.owner=Filters.priority=Filters.status=Filters.storyStatus=Filters.dateFrom=Filters.dateTo=Filters.buildFrom=Filters.buildTo=''; _stageFilter=null; _weekFilter=null; _hoursFilter=null; Router.refresh(); }

/* =====================================================================================
   6) RENDER — shared components
   ===================================================================================== */
function kpiCard(o){
  // o:{label,val,sub,color,icon,accent,click}
  const cl = o.color||'brand';
  const c = CFG.color[cl]||cl;
  return `<div class="kpi ${o.click?'click':''}" ${o.click?`onclick="${o.click}"`:''}>
    <div class="accent" style="background:${c}"></div>
    <div class="k-top">
      <div class="k-label">${esc(o.label)}</div>
      <div class="k-ic" style="background:${c}22;color:${c}">${ico(ICONS[o.icon]||ICONS.exec)}</div>
    </div>
    <div class="k-val" style="color:${o.valColor||'var(--ink)'}">${o.val}</div>
    ${o.sub?`<div class="k-sub">${o.sub}</div>`:''}
    ${o.bar!=null?`<div class="pbar" style="margin-top:11px"><span style="width:${clamp(o.bar,0,100)}%;background:${c}"></span></div>`:''}
  </div>`;
}
function progress(v,color){ color=color||'var(--brand)';
  return `<div class="pwrap"><div class="pbar"><span style="width:${clamp(v||0,0,100)}%;background:${color}"></span></div>
    <span class="pval">${pct(v)}</span></div>`;
}
function stackedBar(segs){ // segs:[{v,color,label}]
  const tot=segs.reduce((a,s)=>a+s.v,0)||1;
  const bars=segs.filter(s=>s.v>0).map(s=>`<span style="width:${s.v/tot*100}%;background:${s.color}" title="${esc(s.label)}: ${s.v}"></span>`).join('');
  const leg=segs.map(s=>`<div class="li"><span class="dot" style="background:${s.color}"></span>${esc(s.label)} <b>${s.v}</b></div>`).join('');
  return `<div class="stack">${bars}</div><div class="legend">${leg}</div>`;
}
function pillFor(kind,x){
  if(kind==='req'){
    if(x.done)return `<span class="pill ok"><span class="d" style="background:var(--ok)"></span>${esc(x.status||'Done')}</span>`;
    if(x.parked)return `<span class="pill neutral"><span class="d" style="background:var(--neutral)"></span>${esc(x.status)}</span>`;
    if(x.isOverdue)return `<span class="pill danger"><span class="d" style="background:var(--danger)"></span>${esc(x.status)}</span>`;
    if(x.backlog)return `<span class="pill info"><span class="d" style="background:var(--info)"></span>${esc(x.status)}</span>`;
    return `<span class="pill warn"><span class="d" style="background:var(--warn)"></span>${esc(x.status)}</span>`;
  }
  if(kind==='story'){
    if(x.blocked)return `<span class="pill danger"><span class="d" style="background:var(--danger)"></span>${esc(x.status||x.stage||'Blocked')}</span>`;
    if(x.done)return `<span class="pill ok"><span class="d" style="background:var(--ok)"></span>${esc(x.status||'Complete')}</span>`;
    if(x.notStarted)return `<span class="pill neutral"><span class="d" style="background:var(--neutral)"></span>Not started</span>`;
    return `<span class="pill warn"><span class="d" style="background:var(--warn)"></span>${esc(x.status||'In progress')}</span>`;
  }
  if(kind==='defect'){
    if(x.resolved)return `<span class="pill ok"><span class="d" style="background:var(--ok)"></span>${esc(x.status)}</span>`;
    return `<span class="pill danger"><span class="d" style="background:var(--danger)"></span>${esc(x.status||'Open')}</span>`;
  }
}
function stagePill(stage){ const c=STAGE_COLOR[stage]||'#6b7889';
  return `<span class="pill" style="background:${c}22;color:${c}"><span class="d" style="background:${c}"></span>${esc(stage||'—')}</span>`; }
function sevPill(sev){ const c={Critical:'--danger',High:'--danger',Medium:'--warn',Low:'--info'}[sev]||'--neutral';
  return `<span class="pill" style="background:var(${c})22"><span class="sev-dot" style="background:var(${c})"></span><span style="color:var(${c})">${esc(sev||'—')}</span></span>`; }
function priPill(p){ const c={Critical:'danger',High:'orange',Medium:'info',Low:'neutral'}[p]||'neutral';
  return `<span class="pill ${c}">${esc(p||'—')}</span>`; }
function compPill(v){ v=Math.round(v||0);
  const c = v>=100?'--ok':v>=75?'--info':v>=50?'--warn':v>=25?'--orange':v>0?'--danger':'--neutral';
  return `<span class="pill" style="background:var(${c})22;color:var(${c})">${v}%</span>`; }
// requirement status → semantic color, and a clickable status KPI widget
function reqStatusColor(status){
  if(CFG.reqDone.includes(status)) return CFG.color.ok;
  if(CFG.reqParked.includes(status)) return CFG.color.neutral;
  if(CFG.reqBacklog.includes(status)) return CFG.color.info;
  return CFG.color.warn;
}
function statusCard(status,count,total,color,active){
  return `<div class="kpi click" onclick="toggleReqStatus('${esc(status)}')"
      style="${active?`border-color:${color};box-shadow:0 0 0 1px ${color} inset`:''}">
    <div class="accent" style="background:${color}"></div>
    <div class="k-top"><div class="k-label" style="line-height:1.35;white-space:normal">${esc(status)}</div>
      <div class="k-ic" style="background:${color}22;color:${color}"><span class="d" style="width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;background:${color};display:block"></span></div></div>
    <div class="k-val" style="color:${color}">${count}</div>
    <div class="pbar" style="margin-top:10px"><span style="width:${total?count/total*100:0}%;background:${color}"></span></div>
  </div>`;
}
function toggleReqStatus(s){ Filters.status = Filters.status===s ? '' : s; Router.refresh(); }
// story stage KPI widgets (facet) — reuse the stage filter used by the pipeline funnel
function toggleStageWidget(s){ _stageFilter = _stageFilter===s ? null : s; Router.refresh(); }
function stageStatCard(stage,count,total,color,active){
  return `<div class="kpi click" onclick="toggleStageWidget('${esc(stage)}')"
      style="${active?`border-color:${color};box-shadow:0 0 0 1px ${color} inset`:''}">
    <div class="accent" style="background:${color}"></div>
    <div class="k-top"><div class="k-label" style="line-height:1.35;white-space:normal">${esc(stage)}</div>
      <div class="k-ic" style="background:${color}22;color:${color}"><span style="width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;background:${color};display:block"></span></div></div>
    <div class="k-val" style="color:${color}">${count}</div>
    <div class="pbar" style="margin-top:10px"><span style="width:${total?count/total*100:0}%;background:${color}"></span></div>
  </div>`;
}
function storyStageWidgets(){
  const base=WS.stories(); // reflects module/owner/priority/date + active Status (stage applied after)
  const counts={}; base.forEach(s=>{ const st=s.stage||'—'; counts[st]=(counts[st]||0)+1; });
  const order=[...CFG.stageOrder, ...Object.keys(counts).filter(k=>!CFG.stageOrder.includes(k))].filter(k=>counts[k]);
  if(!order.length) return '';
  const total=base.length;
  return `<div class="page-head" style="margin:22px 0 12px"><div>
      <h2 style="font-size:15px;margin:0">By Stage</h2>
      <p style="margin:0">click a stage to filter the dashboard · ${order.length} stages</p></div>
      ${_stageFilter?`<button class="clearf" onclick="setStageFilter(null)">✕ ${esc(_stageFilter)}</button>`:''}</div>
    <div class="grid" style="grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(160px,1fr));gap:12px">`+
    order.map(st=>facetCard(st,counts[st],total,STAGE_COLOR[st]||'#6b7889',_stageFilter===st,`toggleStageWidget('${esc(st)}')`)).join('')+`</div>`;
}
// story Status facet widgets
function storyStatusColor(status){
  if(status==='Complete') return CFG.color.ok;
  if(status==='Blocked'||status==='Waiting - Dependency') return CFG.color.danger;
  if(status==='NOT Started'||status==='(blank)') return CFG.color.neutral;
  if(status==='In Progress') return CFG.color.warn;
  return CFG.color.info;
}
function toggleStoryStatus(s){ Filters.storyStatus = Filters.storyStatus===s ? '' : s; Router.refresh(); }
function storiesBaseForFacets(){ const s=Filters.storyStatus; Filters.storyStatus=''; const base=WS.stories(); Filters.storyStatus=s; return base; }
function facetCard(label,count,total,color,active,onclick){
  return `<div class="kpi click" onclick="${onclick}" style="${active?`border-color:${color};box-shadow:0 0 0 1px ${color} inset`:''}">
    <div class="accent" style="background:${color}"></div>
    <div class="k-top"><div class="k-label" style="line-height:1.35;white-space:normal">${esc(label)}</div>
      <div class="k-ic" style="background:${color}22;color:${color}"><span style="width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;background:${color};display:block"></span></div></div>
    <div class="k-val" style="color:${color}">${count}</div>
    <div class="pbar" style="margin-top:10px"><span style="width:${total?count/total*100:0}%;background:${color}"></span></div>
  </div>`;
}
function storyStatusWidgets(){
  const s=Filters.storyStatus; Filters.storyStatus=''; let base=WS.stories(); Filters.storyStatus=s;
  if(_stageFilter) base=base.filter(x=>x.stage===_stageFilter); // reflect the selected Stage
  const counts={}; base.forEach(s=>{ const st=s.status||'(blank)'; counts[st]=(counts[st]||0)+1; });
  const order=Object.keys(counts).sort((a,b)=>counts[b]-counts[a]);
  if(!order.length) return '';
  const total=base.length;
  return `<div class="page-head" style="margin:22px 0 12px"><div>
      <h2 style="font-size:15px;margin:0">By Status</h2>
      <p style="margin:0">click a status to filter the dashboard · ${order.length} statuses</p></div>
      ${Filters.storyStatus?`<button class="clearf" onclick="toggleStoryStatus('${esc(Filters.storyStatus)}')">✕ ${esc(Filters.storyStatus)}</button>`:''}</div>
    <div class="grid" style="grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(160px,1fr));gap:12px">`+
    order.map(st=>facetCard(st,counts[st],total,storyStatusColor(st),Filters.storyStatus===st,`toggleStoryStatus('${esc(st)}')`)).join('')+`</div>`;
}
// facet of Req Status counts (ignores the active status filter so all options stay visible)
function reqStatusWidgets(){
  const saved=Filters.status; Filters.status='';
  const base=WS.req(); Filters.status=saved;
  const counts={}; base.forEach(r=>{ const st=r.status||'—'; counts[st]=(counts[st]||0)+1; });
  const order=Object.keys(counts).sort((a,b)=>counts[b]-counts[a]);
  if(!order.length) return '';
  const total=base.length;
  return `<div class="page-head" style="margin:22px 0 12px"><div>
      <h2 style="font-size:15px;margin:0">By Requirement Status</h2>
      <p style="margin:0">click a status to filter the dashboard · ${order.length} statuses</p></div>
      ${Filters.status?`<button class="clearf" onclick="toggleReqStatus('${esc(Filters.status)}')">✕ ${esc(Filters.status)}</button>`:''}</div>
    <div class="grid" style="grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(190px,1fr));gap:12px">`+
    order.map(st=>statusCard(st,counts[st],total,reqStatusColor(st),Filters.status===st)).join('')+`</div>`;
}

/* sortable data table with row click */
function dataTable(id,cols,rows,rowClick){
  // cols:[{h,key,cls,render,sort}]  rows: array of item objects
  const st = dataTable._sort[id]||{key:null,dir:1};
  if(st.key){ const col=cols.find(c=>c.key===st.key);
    rows=[...rows].sort((a,b)=>{ let x=col.sortVal?col.sortVal(a):a[st.key], y=col.sortVal?col.sortVal(b):b[st.key];
      if(x==null)x=-Infinity; if(y==null)y=-Infinity;
      if(typeof x==='string')return st.dir*x.localeCompare(y); return st.dir*((x>y)-(x<y)); }); }
  const head=cols.map(c=>`<th onclick="dataTable.sortBy('${id}','${c.key}')" class="${c.cls||''}">${esc(c.h)}${st.key===c.key?`<span class="arr">${st.dir>0?'▲':'▼'}</span>`:''}</th>`).join('');
  const body = rows.length? rows.map((r,i)=>`<tr class="${rowClick?'click':''}" ${rowClick?`onclick="${rowClick}(${JSON.stringify(r._kind)},'${esc(r.code||keyOf(r.id))}')"`:''}>`+
      cols.map(c=>`<td class="${c.cls||''}">${c.render?c.render(r):esc(nz(r[c.key]))}</td>`).join('')+`</tr>`).join('')
    : `<tr><td colspan="${cols.length}"><div class="empty">${ico(ICONS.check)}<br>No matching records</div></td></tr>`;
  return `<div class="tablewrap"><table><thead><tr>${head}</tr></thead><tbody>${body}</tbody></table></div>`;
}
dataTable._sort={};
dataTable.sortBy=function(id,key){ const s=dataTable._sort[id]||{key:null,dir:1};
  dataTable._sort[id]= s.key===key?{key,dir:-s.dir}:{key,dir:1}; Router.refresh(); };

/* chart helper (Chart.js) — tracks instances per canvas for teardown */
const Charts={ pool:{}, make(id,cfg){ if(typeof Chart==='undefined')return;
  const cv=document.getElementById(id); if(!cv)return;
  if(this.pool[id]){this.pool[id].destroy();}
  Chart.defaults.color=CFG.color.ink2; Chart.defaults.font.family="'Inter',sans-serif"; Chart.defaults.font.size=11;
  this.pool[id]=new Chart(cv,cfg); return this.pool[id]; },
  clear(){ Object.values(this.pool).forEach(c=>c.destroy()); this.pool={}; } };

function donut(id,labels,data,colors,centerBig,centerLbl,h='h240'){
  setTimeout(()=>Charts.make(id,{type:'doughnut',
    data:{labels,datasets:[{data,backgroundColor:colors,borderColor:'#12161f',borderWidth:2,hoverOffset:5}]},
    options:{cutout:'70%',plugins:{legend:{display:false},tooltip:{callbacks:{label:c=>` ${c.label}: ${c.parsed}`}}},
      maintainAspectRatio:false}}),0);
  const leg=labels.map((l,i)=>`<div class="li"><span class="dot" style="background:${colors[i]}"></span>${esc(l)} <b>${data[i]}</b></div>`).join('');
  return `<div class="chart-box ${h}"><canvas id="${id}"></canvas>
    ${centerBig!=null?`<div class="donut-center"><div class="big">${centerBig}</div><div class="lbl">${esc(centerLbl||'')}</div></div>`:''}</div>
    <div class="legend">${leg}</div>`;
}
function barChart(id,labels,datasets,h='h240',stacked=false,horizontal=false){
  setTimeout(()=>Charts.make(id,{type:'bar',
    data:{labels,datasets:datasets.map(d=>({...d,borderRadius:4,borderSkipped:false}))},
    options:{indexAxis:horizontal?'y':'x',maintainAspectRatio:false,
      plugins:{legend:{display:datasets.length>1,labels:{boxWidth:10,boxHeight:10,padding:12}}},
      scales:{x:{stacked,grid:{color:CFG.color.grid,drawBorder:false},ticks:{maxRotation:0,autoSkip:true}},
        y:{stacked,grid:{color:CFG.color.grid,drawBorder:false},beginAtZero:true}}}}),0);
  return `<div class="chart-box ${h}"><canvas id="${id}"></canvas></div>`;
}
function lineChart(id,labels,datasets,h='h240'){
  setTimeout(()=>Charts.make(id,{type:'line',
    data:{labels,datasets:datasets.map(d=>({...d,tension:.35,borderWidth:2,pointRadius:3,pointHoverRadius:5,fill:d.fill??false}))},
    options:{maintainAspectRatio:false,plugins:{legend:{labels:{boxWidth:10,boxHeight:10,padding:12}}},
      scales:{x:{grid:{color:CFG.color.grid,drawBorder:false}},y:{grid:{color:CFG.color.grid,drawBorder:false},beginAtZero:true}}}}),0);
  return `<div class="chart-box ${h}"><canvas id="${id}"></canvas></div>`;
}

/* SVG semicircle gauge */
function gaugeSVG(value,label,bands){
  value=clamp(value||0,0,100);
  const R=80,cx=100,cy=95, a=Math.PI*(1-value/100);
  const x=cx+R*Math.cos(a), y=cy-R*Math.sin(a);
  let col=CFG.color.ok; if(value<50)col=CFG.color.danger; else if(value<75)col=CFG.color.warn;
  const arc=(f,t,c,w)=>{ const a0=Math.PI*(1-f/100),a1=Math.PI*(1-t/100);
    const x0=cx+R*Math.cos(a0),y0=cy-R*Math.sin(a0),x1=cx+R*Math.cos(a1),y1=cy-R*Math.sin(a1);
    return `<path d="M ${x0} ${y0} A ${R} ${R} 0 0 1 ${x1} ${y1}" fill="none" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="${w}" stroke-linecap="round"/>`; };
  return `<div class="gauge"><svg viewBox="0 0 200 115">
    ${arc(0,100,'#212a3a',13)}
    ${arc(0,value,col,13)}
    <circle cx="${x}" cy="${y}" r="7" fill="${col}" stroke="#12161f" stroke-width="2.5"/>
  </svg>
  <div class="gval" style="color:${col}">${Math.round(value)}</div>
  <div class="glbl">${esc(label)}</div></div>`;
}

/* ageing heatmap: rows = streams, cols = buckets */
function heatmap(matrix){ // matrix:[{label,cells:[{n,color,items,onclick}]}]
  const heads=CFG.ageBuckets.map(b=>`<th>${b.k}d</th>`).join('');
  const maxN=Math.max(1,...matrix.flatMap(r=>r.cells.map(c=>c.n)));
  const rows=matrix.map(r=>`<tr><td>${esc(r.label)}</td>`+r.cells.map((c,i)=>{
    const intensity=c.n/maxN; const base=r.color||'#5aa9ff';
    const bg=c.n===0?'transparent':base+Math.round(30+intensity*200).toString(16).padStart(2,'0');
    return `<td class="cell ${c.n===0?'z':''}" style="background:${bg}" onclick="${c.onclick||''}">${c.n||'·'}</td>`;
  }).join('')+`</tr>`).join('');
  return `<table class="heat"><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">Stream</th>${heads}</tr></thead><tbody>${rows}</tbody></table>`;
}

/* SDLC pipeline funnel for stories */
function pipelineFunnel(stories){
  const counts={}; CFG.stageOrder.forEach(s=>counts[s]=0);
  stories.forEach(s=>{ if(counts[s.stage]!=null)counts[s.stage]++; });
  const max=Math.max(1,...Object.values(counts));
  return `<div class="funnel">`+CFG.stageOrder.map(s=>{
    const n=counts[s], c=STAGE_COLOR[s];
    return `<div class="fstage"><div class="fn">${esc(s)}</div>
      <div class="fbar" onclick="Router.go('stories');setStageFilter('${esc(s)}')"><span style="width:${n/max*100}%;background:${c}"></span></div>
      <div class="fv" style="color:${c}">${n}</div></div>`;
  }).join('')+`</div>`;
}
let _stageFilter=null; function setStageFilter(s){ _stageFilter=s; Router.refresh(); }
let _hoursFilter=null; // null | 'planned' | 'actual' — filter stories to those with hours logged
function filterStoriesByHours(m){ _hoursFilter = _hoursFilter===m ? null : m; Router.refresh();
  toast(_hoursFilter==='actual'?'Showing stories with actual hours':_hoursFilter==='planned'?'Showing stories with planned hours':'Hours filter cleared'); }
let _weekFilter=null; function setWeekFilter(w){ _weekFilter=w; Router.refresh(); }
// per-requirement tracking-screen filters (Stage / Status) for the User Story Tracking list
let _trackStage=null, _trackStatus=null;
function setTrackStage(v){ _trackStage=v||null; Router.refresh(); }
function setTrackStatus(v){ _trackStatus=v||null; Router.refresh(); }
function clearTrackFilter(){ _trackStage=null; _trackStatus=null; Router.refresh(); }

/* completion % breakdown (clickable band → item list) */
const COMP_BANDS=[
  {k:'Not started · 0%',lo:0,hi:0,c:'#6b7889'},
  {k:'1 – 25%',lo:1,hi:25,c:'#ff5c6c'},
