  {k:'26 – 50%',lo:26,hi:50,c:'#ff924d'},
  {k:'51 – 75%',lo:51,hi:75,c:'#f0b429'},
  {k:'76 – 99%',lo:76,hi:99,c:'#5aa9ff'},
  {k:'Complete · 100%',lo:100,hi:100,c:'#39d98a'},
];
function completionDist(list){ return COMP_BANDS.map(b=>{
  const items=list.filter(x=>{const v=Math.round(x.comp||0); return v>=b.lo&&v<=b.hi;});
  return {...b,n:items.length,items}; }); }
compDrill._data={};
function compCard(scope,title,list){
  const dist=completionDist(list); compDrill._data[scope]=dist;
  const max=Math.max(1,...dist.map(d=>d.n)), tot=list.length||1, done=dist[dist.length-1].n;
  const rows=dist.map((d,i)=>`<div class="fstage" style="grid-template-columns:150px 1fr 96px">
    <div class="fn">${esc(d.k)}</div>
    <div class="fbar" onclick="compDrill('${scope}',${i})"><span style="width:${d.n/max*100}%;background:${d.c}"></span></div>
    <div class="fv" style="color:${d.c}">${d.n} <span class="dim" style="font-weight:500">${Math.round(d.n/tot*100)}%</span></div>
  </div>`).join('');
  return `<div class="card"><div class="card-h"><h3>${esc(title)}</h3>
      <span class="sub">${done}/${tot} at 100% · click a band to list</span></div>
    <div class="funnel">${rows}</div><div id="compDrill_${scope}"></div></div>`;
}
function compDrill(scope,i){ const d=compDrill._data[scope][i]; const box=document.getElementById('compDrill_'+scope); if(!box)return;
  const col = scope==='req'?'var(--violet)':'var(--blue)';
  box.innerHTML=`<div class="dsection">${esc(d.k)} · ${d.n} item(s)</div>`+
    (d.items.map(x=>`<div class="linkrow" onclick="openDrawer('${scope}','${esc(keyOf(x.id))}')">
      <span class="code" style="color:${col}">${esc(x.code)}</span>
      <span class="lt">${esc(x.subject)}</span><span class="pill neutral">${pct(x.comp)}</span></div>`).join('')||'<div class="empty">None</div>');
  box.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'nearest'});
}

/* =====================================================================================
   6b) RENDER — view renderers
   ===================================================================================== */
const Views={};

/* ---------- shared header ---------- */
function pageHead(title,sub){ return `<div class="page-head"><div><h2>${esc(title)}</h2><p>${esc(sub)}</p></div></div>`; }
function insightTile(n,label,color,icon,click){ const c=CFG.color[color]||color;
  return `<div class="insight" onclick="${click||''}"><div class="ic" style="background:${c}22;color:${c}">${ico(ICONS[icon]||ICONS.alert)}</div>
    <div><div class="n" style="color:${c}">${n}</div><div class="t">${esc(label)}</div></div></div>`; }

/* ================= EXECUTIVE ================= */
Views.exec=function(){
  const o=Engine.overall(); const health=Engine.portfolioHealth();
  const req=o.req, st=o.story, de=o.defect;
  // due today / this week across streams
  const AS=Store.asOf; const inDays=(d,lo,hi)=>{const n=daysBetween(AS,d); return n!=null&&n>=lo&&n<=hi;};
  const openReq=Store.req.filter(r=>!r.done&&!r.parked), openStory=Store.stories.filter(s=>!s.done&&!s.blocked);
  const dueToday=[...openReq,...openStory].filter(x=>x.due&&daysBetween(AS,x.due)===0).length;
  const dueWeek=[...openReq,...openStory].filter(x=>x.due&&inDays(x.due,0,7)).length;
  const upcoming=Store.stories.filter(s=>!s.done&&s.due&&inDays(s.due,0,14)).length;
  const risks=Engine.reqRollup().filter(x=>!x.req.parked).sort((a,b)=>b.risk.score-a.risk.score).slice(0,6);

  const kpis=[
    kpiCard({label:'Overall SDLC Completion',val:o.val+'<small>%</small>',color:'brand',icon:'exec',bar:o.val,
      sub:`<span class="muted">${req.done+st.done} of ${req.t+st.t} work items delivered</span>`}),
    kpiCard({label:'Requirement Completion',val:req.comp+'<small>%</small>',color:'violet',icon:'req',bar:req.comp,
      sub:`<span class="muted">${req.done}/${req.t} in production · ${req.over} overdue</span>`,click:"Router.go('requirements')"}),
    kpiCard({label:'User Story Completion',val:st.comp+'<small>%</small>',color:'blue',icon:'story',bar:st.comp,
      sub:`<span class="muted">${st.done}/${st.t} done · ${st.blocked} blocked</span>`,click:"Router.go('stories')"}),
    kpiCard({label:'Defect Resolution',val:de.pctResolved+'<small>%</small>',color:'green',icon:'defect',bar:de.pctResolved,
      sub:`<span class="muted">${de.resolved}/${de.t} resolved · ${de.crit} open critical</span>`,click:"Router.go('defects')"}),
    kpiCard({label:'Hours Tracking',val:st.totalA+'<small>/'+st.totalP+'h</small>',color:'orange',icon:'clock',bar:st.hoursPct,
      sub:`<span class="muted">${st.hoursPct}% logged · Wk: ${st.weekA}/${st.weekP}h</span>`}),
    kpiCard({label:'Portfolio Health',val:health.score,valColor:health.score>=75?'var(--ok)':health.score>=50?'var(--warn)':'var(--danger)',
      color:health.score>=75?'green':health.score>=50?'warn':'danger',icon:'check',bar:health.score,
      sub:`<span class="pill ${health.band==='Healthy'?'ok':health.band==='At Risk'?'warn':'danger'}">${health.band}</span> <span class="muted">${health.atRisk} at risk</span>`,
      click:"Router.go('risk')"}),
  ].join('');

  const insights=[
    insightTile(req.over,'Overdue Requirements','danger','alert',"Router.go('requirements')"),
    insightTile(st.blocked,'Blocked Stories','danger','block',"Router.go('stories')"),
    insightTile(st.qa,'Pending QA','warn','check',"Router.go('stories')"),
    insightTile(st.dep,'Pending Deployment','info','deploy',"Router.go('stories')"),
    insightTile(de.crit,'Open Critical Defects','danger','fire',"Router.go('defects')"),
    insightTile(health.atRisk,'Projects at Risk','orange','risk',"Router.go('risk')"),
    insightTile(dueWeek,'Due This Week','warn','clock',"Router.go('risk')"),
    insightTile(dueToday,'Items Due Today','violet','clock',"Router.go('risk')"),
  ].join('');

  // requirement status donut
  const rs=Store.req.reduce((m,r)=>{const k=r.done?'Complete':r.parked?'Parked':r.backlog?'Backlog':r.isOverdue?'Overdue':'In Progress';m[k]=(m[k]||0)+1;return m;},{});
  const rsL=['Complete','In Progress','Backlog','Overdue','Parked'], rsC=[CFG.color.ok,CFG.color.warn,CFG.color.info,CFG.color.danger,CFG.color.neutral];
  const wk=Engine.weekly();

  return pageHead('Executive Dashboard','End-to-end delivery health across requirements, stories, and defects')
    + `<div class="grid g-kpi">${kpis}</div>`
    + `<div class="grid g-4 mt">${insights}</div>`
    + `<div class="grid g-3 mt">
        <div class="card"><div class="card-h"><h3>Requirement Status</h3><span class="sub">${req.t} total</span></div>
          ${donut('exReqDonut',rsL,rsL.map(l=>rs[l]||0),rsC,req.comp+'%','Complete')}</div>
        <div class="card"><div class="card-h"><h3>User Story Pipeline</h3><span class="sub">${st.t} stories</span></div>
          ${pipelineFunnel(Store.stories)}</div>
        <div class="card"><div class="card-h"><h3>Top Risk Requirements</h3><span class="sub">by risk score</span></div>
          ${risks.map(x=>`<div class="linkrow" onclick="openDrawer('req','${esc(x.req.code)}')">
            <span class="code">${esc(x.req.code)}</span><span class="lt">${esc(x.req.subject)}</span>
            <span class="pill ${x.risk.score>=60?'danger':x.risk.score>=35?'warn':'neutral'}">${x.risk.score}</span></div>`).join('')||'<div class="empty">No risks flagged</div>'}
        </div>
      </div>`
    + `<div class="grid g-2 mt">
        <div class="card"><div class="card-h"><h3>Weekly Delivery Trend</h3><span class="sub">stories completed vs planned</span></div>
          ${barChart('exWeekly',wk.map(w=>'W'+w.wk),[
            {label:'Completed',data:wk.map(w=>w.done),backgroundColor:CFG.color.brand},
            {label:'Planned',data:wk.map(w=>w.planned),backgroundColor:CFG.color.grid}],'h240',false)}</div>
        <div class="card"><div class="card-h"><h3>Defect Overview</h3><span class="sub">${de.t} defects</span></div>
          ${donut('exDefDonut',['Resolved','Open'],[de.resolved,de.open],[CFG.color.ok,CFG.color.danger],de.pctResolved+'%','Resolved')}</div>
      </div>`;
};

/* ================= REQUIREMENTS ================= */
Views.requirements=function(){
  const list=WS.req(); const s=Engine.reqStats(list);
  const kpis=[
    kpiCard({label:'Total Requirements',val:s.t,color:'violet',icon:'req'}),
    kpiCard({label:'Completion',val:s.comp+'<small>%</small>',color:'brand',icon:'check',bar:s.comp,sub:`<span class="muted">${s.done} in production</span>`}),
    kpiCard({label:'In Progress',val:s.prog,color:'warn',icon:'clock',sub:`<span class="muted">${s.back} in backlog</span>`}),
    kpiCard({label:'Overdue',val:s.over,color:'danger',icon:'alert',valColor:s.over?'var(--danger)':'var(--ink)'}),
    kpiCard({label:'Deferred / Hold',val:s.parked,color:'neutral',icon:'block'}),
  ].join('');
  // charts
  const byPri=['Critical','High','Medium','Low'].map(p=>list.filter(r=>r.priority===p).length);
  const byMod={}; list.forEach(r=>byMod[r.module]=(byMod[r.module]||0)+1);
  const modL=Object.keys(byMod).sort((a,b)=>byMod[b]-byMod[a]).slice(0,10);
  const byType={}; list.forEach(r=>byType[r.type]=(byType[r.type]||0)+1);
  const cols=[
    {h:'ID',key:'code',cls:'',render:r=>reqTrackLink(r.id,r.code)},
    {h:'Subject',key:'subject',cls:'tt',render:r=>esc(r.subject)},
    {h:'Module',key:'module'},
    {h:'Type',key:'type',render:r=>`<span class="pill neutral">${esc(nz(r.type))}</span>`},
    {h:'Priority',key:'priority',sortVal:r=>PRIORITY_RANK[r.priority]||0,render:r=>priPill(r.priority)},
    {h:'SDLC Req Owner',key:'owner'},
    {h:'Completion',key:'comp',cls:'',render:r=>progress(r.comp, r.done?'var(--ok)':'var(--brand)')},
    {h:'Status',key:'status',render:r=>pillFor('req',r)},
    {h:'Due (Expected)',key:'expectedDate',sortVal:r=>r.expectedDate?+pd(r.expectedDate):Infinity,render:r=>{
        if(!r.expectedDate) return '<span class="dim">—</span>';
        return r.isOverdue?`<span class="pill danger">${fmtShort(r.expectedDate)} · ${r.overdue}d late</span>`:`<span class="mono">${fmtShort(r.expectedDate)}</span>`;
      }},
    {h:'Revised Delivery',key:'revisedDate',sortVal:r=>r.revisedDate?+pd(r.revisedDate):Infinity,render:r=>r.revisedDate?`<span class="mono" style="color:var(--orange)">${fmtShort(r.revisedDate)}</span>`:'<span class="dim">—</span>'},
    {h:'Remarks',key:'remarks',cls:'tt',render:r=>r.remarks?`<span title="${esc(r.remarks)}">${esc(r.remarks)}</span>`:'<span class="dim">—</span>'},
  ];
  return pageHead('SDLC Requirement Dashboard','Requirement lifecycle, priority, and delivery status')
    + renderFilterBar(['req'])
    + `<div class="grid g-kpi">${kpis}</div>`
    + reqStatusWidgets(list)
    + `<div class="grid g-3 mt">
        <div class="card"><div class="card-h"><h3>By Priority</h3></div>${barChart('reqPri',['Critical','High','Medium','Low'],[{label:'Requirements',data:byPri,backgroundColor:[CFG.color.danger,CFG.color.orange,CFG.color.info,CFG.color.neutral]}],'h220')}</div>
        <div class="card"><div class="card-h"><h3>By Module</h3></div>${barChart('reqMod',modL,[{label:'Requirements',data:modL.map(m=>byMod[m]),backgroundColor:CFG.color.violet}],'h220',false,true)}</div>
        <div class="card"><div class="card-h"><h3>By Type</h3></div>${donut('reqType',Object.keys(byType),Object.values(byType),[CFG.color.brand,CFG.color.violet,CFG.color.blue,CFG.color.orange,CFG.color.pink,CFG.color.green],null,null,'h200')}</div>
      </div>`
    + `<div class="mt">`+compCard('req','Requirement Completion Breakdown',list)+`</div>`
    + `<div class="card mt"><div class="card-h"><h3>All Requirements</h3><span class="sub">click a row for full detail & linked stories</span></div>
        ${dataTable('reqTable',cols,list,'openDrawer')}</div>`;
};

/* ================= USER STORIES ================= */
Views.stories=function(){
  let list=WS.stories(); if(_stageFilter) list=list.filter(s=>s.stage===_stageFilter);
  if(_weekFilter!=null) list=list.filter(s=>s.weekNo===_weekFilter);
  if(_hoursFilter==='planned') list=list.filter(s=>s.totalPlanned!=null);
  if(_hoursFilter==='actual') list=list.filter(s=>s.totalActual!=null);
  const s=Engine.storyStats(list);
  const kpis=[
    kpiCard({label:'Total Stories',val:s.t,color:'blue',icon:'story'}),
    kpiCard({label:'Completion',val:s.comp+'<small>%</small>',color:'brand',icon:'check',bar:s.comp,sub:`<span class="muted">${s.done} done</span>`}),
    kpiCard({label:'Blocked',val:s.blocked,color:'danger',icon:'block',valColor:s.blocked?'var(--danger)':'var(--ink)'}),
    kpiCard({label:'Pending QA / Deploy',val:s.qa+' / '+s.dep,color:'warn',icon:'deploy'}),
    kpiCard({label:'This Week Planned',val:s.weekP+'<small>h</small>',color:'violet',icon:'clock',sub:`<span class=\"muted\">${s.withWP} stories this week</span>`}),
    kpiCard({label:'This Week Actual',val:s.weekA+'<small>/'+s.weekP+'h</small>',valColor:s.weekP&&s.weekA>s.weekP?'var(--danger)':'var(--ink)',color:s.weekP&&s.weekA>s.weekP?'danger':'brand',icon:'clock',bar:s.weekPct,sub:`<span class=\"muted\">${s.weekPct}% of weekly plan</span>`}),
  ].join('');
  const cols=[
    {h:'ID',key:'code',render:r=>codeLink('story',r.id,r.code)},
    {h:'Story',key:'subject',cls:'tt',render:r=>esc(r.subject)},
    {h:'Requirement',key:'reqCode',render:r=>reqTrackLinkByCode(r.reqCode,'var(--violet)')},
    {h:'Stage',key:'stage',sortVal:r=>r.stageIdx,render:r=>stagePill(r.stage)},
    {h:'SDLC Req Owner',key:'reqOwner',render:r=>esc(nz(r.reqOwner))},
    {h:'Total Plan',key:'totalPlanned',cls:'tnum',sortVal:r=>r.totalPlanned==null?-1:r.totalPlanned,render:r=>r.totalPlanned!=null?`<span class="mono" style="color:var(--info)">${r.totalPlanned}h</span>`:'<span class="dim">—</span>'},
    {h:'Wk Plan',key:'weekPlanned',cls:'tnum',sortVal:r=>r.weekPlanned==null?-1:r.weekPlanned,render:r=>r.weekPlanned!=null?`<span class="mono" style="color:var(--violet)">${r.weekPlanned}h</span>`:'<span class="dim">\u2014</span>'},
    {h:'Wk Actual',key:'weekActual',cls:'tnum',sortVal:r=>r.weekActual==null?-1:r.weekActual,render:r=>{
        if(r.weekActual==null) return '<span class="dim">\u2014</span>';
        const over=r.weekPlanned!=null&&r.weekActual>r.weekPlanned;
        return `<span class="mono" style="color:${over?'var(--danger)':'var(--brand)'}">${r.weekActual}h</span>`; }},
    {h:'Total Actual',key:'totalActual',cls:'tnum',sortVal:r=>r.totalActual==null?-1:r.totalActual,render:r=>{
        if(r.totalActual==null) return '<span class="dim">—</span>';
        const over=r.totalPlanned!=null&&r.totalActual>r.totalPlanned;
        return `<span class="mono" style="color:${over?'var(--danger)':'var(--ok)'}">${r.totalActual}h</span>`;
      }},
    {h:'Completion',key:'comp',render:r=>progress(r.comp, r.done?'var(--ok)':r.blocked?'var(--danger)':'var(--brand)')},
    {h:'Status',key:'status',render:r=>pillFor('story',r)},
    {h:'Due',key:'due',sortVal:r=>r.due?+pd(r.due):Infinity,render:r=>r.isOverdue?`<span class="pill danger">${r.overdue}d late</span>`:fmtShort(r.due)},
  ];
  const stageBanner=(_stageFilter?`<div class="chipcount" style="margin-bottom:10px">Stage: <b>${esc(_stageFilter)}</b> <button class="clearf" onclick="setStageFilter(null)">✕ clear</button></div>`:'')
    +(Filters.storyStatus?`<div class="chipcount" style="margin-bottom:10px">Status: <b>${esc(Filters.storyStatus)}</b> <button class="clearf" onclick="toggleStoryStatus('${esc(Filters.storyStatus)}')">✕ clear</button></div>`:'')
    +(_weekFilter!=null?`<div class="chipcount" style="margin-bottom:10px">Week: <b>W${_weekFilter}</b> <button class="clearf" onclick="setWeekFilter(null)">✕ clear</button></div>`:'')
    +(_hoursFilter?`<div class="chipcount" style="margin-bottom:10px">Hours: <b>${_hoursFilter==='actual'?'Actual logged':'Planned logged'}</b> <button class="clearf" onclick="filterStoriesByHours('${_hoursFilter}')">✕ clear</button></div>`:'');
  const byOwner={}; list.forEach(x=>byOwner[x.owner]=(byOwner[x.owner]||0)+1);
  const hrsByOwner={}; list.forEach(x=>{ if(x.plannedHours||x.actualHours){ const o=x.owner||'—'; if(!hrsByOwner[o])hrsByOwner[o]={p:0,a:0}; hrsByOwner[o].p+=x.plannedHours||0; hrsByOwner[o].a+=x.actualHours||0; } });
  const hrsOwners=Object.keys(hrsByOwner);
  return pageHead('SDLC User Story Dashboard','Development pipeline, story points, and delivery flow')
    + renderFilterBar(['story'])
    + `<div class="grid g-kpi">${kpis}</div>`
    + storyStageWidgets()
    + storyStatusWidgets()
    + `<div class="card mt">${stageBanner}<div class="card-h"><h3>All User Stories</h3><span class="sub">click a row for detail, parent/child & defects</span></div>
        ${dataTable('storyTable',cols,list,'openDrawer')}</div>`;
};

/* ================= DEFECTS ================= */
Views.defects=function(){
  const list=WS.defects(); const s=Engine.defectStats(list);
  const kpis=[
    kpiCard({label:'Total Defects',val:s.t,color:'green',icon:'defect'}),
    kpiCard({label:'Resolution Rate',val:s.pctResolved+'<small>%</small>',color:'brand',icon:'check',bar:s.pctResolved}),
    kpiCard({label:'Open',val:s.open,color:'danger',icon:'alert',valColor:s.open?'var(--danger)':'var(--ink)'}),
    kpiCard({label:'Open Critical',val:s.crit,color:'danger',icon:'fire',valColor:s.crit?'var(--danger)':'var(--ink)'}),
    kpiCard({label:'Avg Age (days)',val:s.t?Math.round(list.reduce((a,d)=>a+d.age,0)/s.t):0,color:'warn',icon:'age'}),
  ].join('');
  const bySev=['Critical','High','Medium','Low'].map(x=>list.filter(d=>d.severity===x).length);
  const byEnv={}; list.forEach(d=>byEnv[d.environment||'—']=(byEnv[d.environment||'—']||0)+1);
  const cols=[
    {h:'ID',key:'code',render:r=>codeLink('defect',r.id,r.code)},
    {h:'Description',key:'description',cls:'tt',render:r=>esc(r.description)},
    {h:'Severity',key:'severity',sortVal:r=>PRIORITY_RANK[r.severity]||0,render:r=>sevPill(r.severity)},
    {h:'Req',key:'reqCode',render:r=>reqTrackLinkByCode(r.reqCode,'var(--violet)')},
    {h:'Story',key:'storyCode',render:r=>fkCodeLink('story',r.storyCode,'var(--blue)')},
    {h:'Env',key:'environment',render:r=>`<span class="pill neutral">${esc(nz(r.environment))}</span>`},
    {h:'Owner',key:'owner'},
    {h:'Age',key:'age',cls:'tnum',render:r=>`<span class="mono">${r.age}d</span>`},
    {h:'Created',key:'createdTime',sortVal:r=>r.createdTime?+pd(r.createdTime):0,render:r=>fmtShort(r.createdTime)},
    {h:'Closed',key:'expectedDate',sortVal:r=>r.expectedDate?+pd(r.expectedDate):Infinity,render:r=>r.expectedDate?`<span class="mono">${fmtShort(r.expectedDate)}</span>`:'<span class="dim">—</span>'},
    {h:'Status',key:'status',render:r=>pillFor('defect',r)},
  ];
  return pageHead('SDLC Defect Dashboard','Quality, severity, and defect resolution tracking')
    + renderFilterBar(['defect'])
    + `<div class="grid g-kpi">${kpis}</div>`
    + `<div class="card mt"><div class="card-h"><h3>All Defects</h3><span class="sub">click a row for root cause & fix detail</span></div>
        ${dataTable('defTable',cols,list,'openDrawer')}</div>`;
};

/* ================= WEEKLY DELIVERY ================= */
Views.weekly=function(){
  const wk=Engine.weekly();
  const totalDone=wk.reduce((a,w)=>a+w.done,0), totalPlan=wk.reduce((a,w)=>a+w.planned,0);
  const totalPts=wk.reduce((a,w)=>a+w.ptsDone,0), totalDep=wk.reduce((a,w)=>a+w.deployed,0);
  const kpis=[
    kpiCard({label:'Weeks Tracked',val:wk.length,color:'brand',icon:'week'}),
    kpiCard({label:'Stories Delivered',val:totalDone+'<small>/'+totalPlan+'</small>',color:'green',icon:'check',bar:totalPlan?totalDone/totalPlan*100:0}),
    kpiCard({label:'Points Delivered',val:totalPts,color:'orange',icon:'point'}),
    kpiCard({label:'Deployments',val:totalDep,color:'violet',icon:'deploy'}),
    kpiCard({label:'Avg Velocity',val:wk.length?Math.round(totalPts/wk.length):0,color:'blue',icon:'up',sub:'<span class="muted">points / week</span>'}),
  ].join('');
  // burnup: cumulative done vs cumulative planned
  let cd=0,cp=0; const cumDone=[],cumPlan=[];
  wk.forEach(w=>{cd+=w.done;cp+=w.planned;cumDone.push(cd);cumPlan.push(cp);});
  const cols=[
    {h:'Week',key:'wk',render:r=>`<span class="code">W${r.wk}</span>`},
    {h:'Start',key:'start',sortVal:r=>r.start?+pd(r.start):0,render:r=>fmtDate(r.start)},
    {h:'Planned',key:'planned',cls:'tnum',render:r=>`<span class="mono">${r.planned}</span>`},
    {h:'Completed',key:'done',cls:'tnum',render:r=>`<span class="mono" style="color:var(--ok)">${r.done}</span>`},
    {h:'Blocked',key:'blocked',cls:'tnum',render:r=>`<span class="mono" style="color:${r.blocked?'var(--danger)':'var(--ink-3)'}">${r.blocked}</span>`},
    {h:'Points',key:'ptsDone',cls:'tnum',render:r=>`<span class="mono">${r.ptsDone}</span>`},
    {h:'Deployed',key:'deployed',cls:'tnum',render:r=>`<span class="mono">${r.deployed}</span>`},
    {h:'Progress',key:'done',render:r=>progress(r.planned?Math.round(r.done/r.planned*100):0,'var(--brand)')},
  ];
  // week bars (custom stacked done/blocked/other)
  const maxP=Math.max(1,...wk.map(w=>w.planned));
  const bars=wk.map(w=>{const other=w.planned-w.done-w.blocked;
    return `<div class="wkcol" title="W${w.wk}: ${w.done} done / ${w.planned}" onclick="filterWeek(${w.wk})">
      <div class="stackv" style="height:${w.planned/maxP*100}%">
        <span style="height:${w.done/w.planned*100||0}%;background:${CFG.color.ok}"></span>
        <span style="height:${w.blocked/w.planned*100||0}%;background:${CFG.color.danger}"></span>
        <span style="height:${other/w.planned*100||0}%;background:${CFG.color.grid}"></span>
      </div><div class="wl">W${w.wk}</div></div>`;}).join('');
  return pageHead('Weekly Delivery Dashboard','Sprint cadence, velocity, and delivery throughput')
    + `<div class="grid g-kpi">${kpis}</div>`
    + `<div class="grid g-2 mt">
        <div class="card"><div class="card-h"><h3>Weekly Throughput</h3><div class="legend" style="margin:0"><div class="li"><span class="dot" style="background:${CFG.color.ok}"></span>Done</div><div class="li"><span class="dot" style="background:${CFG.color.danger}"></span>Blocked</div><div class="li"><span class="dot" style="background:${CFG.color.grid}"></span>Open</div></div></div>
          <div class="wkchart">${bars||'<div class="empty">No weekly data</div>'}</div></div>
        <div class="card"><div class="card-h"><h3>Delivery Burn-up</h3><span class="sub">cumulative</span></div>
          ${lineChart('wkBurn',wk.map(w=>'W'+w.wk),[
            {label:'Completed',data:cumDone,borderColor:CFG.color.brand,backgroundColor:CFG.color.brand+'22',fill:true},
            {label:'Planned',data:cumPlan,borderColor:CFG.color.info,backgroundColor:'transparent'}],'h240')}</div>
      </div>`
    + `<div class="card mt"><div class="card-h"><h3>Weekly Breakdown</h3></div>${dataTable('wkTable',cols,wk)}</div>`;
};
function filterWeek(w){ Router.go('stories'); _stageFilter=null; _weekFilter=w; Router.refresh();
  toast('Showing stories · Week '+w); }

/* ================= PROJECT PROGRESS ================= */
Views.project=function(){
  const roll=Engine.reqRollup();
  const modMap={}; roll.forEach(x=>{const m=x.req.module||'—'; if(!modMap[m])modMap[m]={mod:m,reqs:0,comp:0,stories:0,done:0,defects:0};
    const g=modMap[m]; g.reqs++; g.comp+=x.comp; g.stories+=x.stories.t; g.done+=x.stories.done; g.defects+=x.defects.t;});
  const mods=Object.values(modMap).map(g=>({...g,comp:Math.round(g.comp/g.reqs)})).sort((a,b)=>b.reqs-a.reqs);
  const kpis=[
    kpiCard({label:'Active Modules',val:mods.length,color:'brand',icon:'proj'}),
    kpiCard({label:'Requirements',val:roll.length,color:'violet',icon:'req'}),
    kpiCard({label:'Avg Completion',val:(roll.length?Math.round(roll.reduce((a,x)=>a+x.comp,0)/roll.length):0)+'<small>%</small>',color:'green',icon:'check'}),
    kpiCard({label:'Fully Delivered',val:roll.filter(x=>x.comp>=100).length,color:'ok',icon:'deploy'}),
  ].join('');
  const cols=[
    {h:'Requirement',key:'code',sortVal:r=>r.req.code,render:r=>reqTrackLink(r.req.id,r.req.code)},
    {h:'Subject',key:'subject',cls:'tt',sortVal:r=>r.req.subject,render:r=>esc(r.req.subject)},
    {h:'Module',key:'module',sortVal:r=>r.req.module,render:r=>esc(nz(r.req.module))},
    {h:'Stories',key:'stories',cls:'tnum',sortVal:r=>r.stories.t,render:r=>`<span class="mono">${r.stories.done}/${r.stories.t}</span>`},
    {h:'Points',key:'pts',cls:'tnum',sortVal:r=>r.stories.est,render:r=>`<span class="mono">${r.stories.cons}/${r.stories.est}</span>`},
    {h:'Defects',key:'defects',cls:'tnum',sortVal:r=>r.defects.t,render:r=>`<span class="mono" style="color:${r.defects.crit?'var(--danger)':'var(--ink-2)'}">${r.defects.open}/${r.defects.t}</span>`},
    {h:'Progress',key:'comp',sortVal:r=>r.comp,render:r=>progress(r.comp,r.comp>=100?'var(--ok)':'var(--brand)')},
    {h:'Health',key:'health',cls:'tnum',sortVal:r=>r.health,render:r=>`<span class="pill ${r.health>=75?'ok':r.health>=50?'warn':'danger'}">${r.health}</span>`},
  ];
  const rows=roll.map(r=>({...r,_kind:'req',id:r.req.id}));
  return pageHead('Project Progress Dashboard','Requirement-level rollup: stories, points, defects & health')
    + renderFilterBar(['req'])
    + `<div class="grid g-kpi">${kpis}</div>`
    + `<div class="card mt"><div class="card-h"><h3>Progress by Module</h3></div>
        ${barChart('projMod',mods.map(m=>m.mod),[{label:'Avg Completion %',data:mods.map(m=>m.comp),backgroundColor:mods.map(m=>m.comp>=75?CFG.color.ok:m.comp>=40?CFG.color.warn:CFG.color.danger)}],'h260',false,true)}</div>`
    + `<div class="card mt"><div class="card-h"><h3>Requirement Rollup</h3><span class="sub">click a row to drill into stories & defects</span></div>
        ${dataTable('projTable',cols,rows,'openDrawer')}</div>`;
};

/* ================= DEVELOPER PRODUCTIVITY ================= */
Views.dev=function(){
  const p=Engine.productivity();
  const kpis=[
    kpiCard({label:'Contributors',val:p.length,color:'brand',icon:'people'}),
    kpiCard({label:'Points Delivered',val:p.reduce((a,x)=>a+x.ptsDone,0),color:'orange',icon:'point'}),
    kpiCard({label:'Stories Completed',val:p.reduce((a,x)=>a+x.done,0),color:'green',icon:'check'}),
    kpiCard({label:'Defects Fixed',val:p.reduce((a,x)=>a+x.defectsFixed,0),color:'violet',icon:'defect'}),
  ].join('');
  const cols=[
    {h:'Team Member',key:'name'},
    {h:'Owned',key:'owned',cls:'tnum',render:r=>`<span class="mono">${r.owned}</span>`},
    {h:'Completed',key:'done',cls:'tnum',render:r=>`<span class="mono" style="color:var(--ok)">${r.done}</span>`},
    {h:'In Progress',key:'inProg',cls:'tnum',render:r=>`<span class="mono">${r.inProg}</span>`},
    {h:'Blocked',key:'blocked',cls:'tnum',render:r=>`<span class="mono" style="color:${r.blocked?'var(--danger)':'var(--ink-3)'}">${r.blocked}</span>`},
    {h:'Pts Done',key:'ptsDone',cls:'tnum',render:r=>`<span class="mono">${r.ptsDone}</span>`},
    {h:'Defects Fixed',key:'defectsFixed',cls:'tnum',render:r=>`<span class="mono">${r.defectsFixed}</span>`},
    {h:'Avg Cycle',key:'avgCycle',cls:'tnum',render:r=>`<span class="mono">${r.avgCycle!=null?r.avgCycle+'d':'—'}</span>`},
    {h:'Throughput',key:'pctDone',render:r=>progress(r.pctDone,'var(--brand)')},
  ];
  return pageHead('Developer Productivity Dashboard','Ownership, throughput, velocity & quality contribution')
    + `<div class="grid g-kpi">${kpis}</div>`
    + `<div class="grid g-2 mt">
        <div class="card"><div class="card-h"><h3>Points Delivered by Member</h3></div>
          ${barChart('devPts',p.map(x=>x.name),[
            {label:'Points Done',data:p.map(x=>x.ptsDone),backgroundColor:CFG.color.brand},
            {label:'Points Total',data:p.map(x=>x.pts),backgroundColor:CFG.color.grid}],'h260',false,true)}</div>
        <div class="card"><div class="card-h"><h3>Story Status Mix</h3></div>
          ${barChart('devMix',p.map(x=>x.name),[
            {label:'Done',data:p.map(x=>x.done),backgroundColor:CFG.color.ok},
            {label:'In Progress',data:p.map(x=>x.inProg),backgroundColor:CFG.color.warn},
            {label:'Blocked',data:p.map(x=>x.blocked),backgroundColor:CFG.color.danger}],'h260',true,true)}</div>
      </div>`
    + `<div class="card mt"><div class="card-h"><h3>Productivity Detail</h3></div>${dataTable('devTable',cols,p)}</div>`;
};

/* ================= AGEING ================= */
Views.age=function(){
  const openDef=d=>d.open;
  const opens=Store.defects.filter(openDef);
  const ds=Engine.defectStats(Store.defects);
  const avgAge=opens.length?Math.round(opens.reduce((a,d)=>a+d.age,0)/opens.length):0;
  const aged30=opens.filter(d=>d.age>30).length;
  const kpis=[
    kpiCard({label:'Open Defects',val:opens.length,color:'orange',icon:'defect'}),
    kpiCard({label:'Open Critical',val:Store.defects.filter(d=>d.openCritical).length,color:'danger',icon:'fire',valColor:Store.defects.filter(d=>d.openCritical).length?'var(--danger)':'var(--ink)'}),
    kpiCard({label:'Aged 30+ days',val:aged30,color:'danger',icon:'age',valColor:aged30?'var(--danger)':'var(--ink)'}),
    kpiCard({label:'Avg Age (open)',val:avgAge+'<small>d</small>',color:'warn',icon:'clock'}),
    kpiCard({label:'Resolved',val:ds.resolved+'<small>/'+ds.t+'</small>',color:'green',icon:'check',bar:ds.pctResolved}),
  ].join('');
  // ageing matrix: rows = severity, cols = days-open buckets (open defects only)
  const sevs=[['Critical',CFG.color.danger],['High',CFG.color.orange],['Medium',CFG.color.warn],['Low',CFG.color.info]];
  ageDrill._data={};
  const matrix=sevs.map(([sev,hex])=>{
    const buckets=Engine.ageing(Store.defects, d=>d.open&&d.severity===sev);
    ageDrill._data[sev]=buckets;
    return {label:sev, color:hex, cells:buckets.map((b,i)=>({n:b.n,onclick:`ageDrill('${sev}',${i})`}))};
  });
  // age distribution across all open defects
  const dist=Engine.ageing(Store.defects,openDef);
  const oldest=[...opens].sort((a,b)=>b.age-a.age).slice(0,10);
  const oldRow=x=>`<div class="linkrow" onclick="openDrawer('defect','${esc(x.code)}')">
    <span class="code" style="color:var(--orange)">${esc(x.code)}</span><span class="lt">${esc(x.description)}</span>
    ${sevPill(x.severity)}<span class="pill ${x.age>60?'danger':x.age>30?'warn':'neutral'}">${x.age}d</span></div>`;
  return pageHead('Defect Ageing Dashboard','How long open defects have been sitting — by severity and days-open')
    + `<div class="grid g-kpi">${kpis}</div>`
    + `<div class="card mt"><div class="card-h"><h3>Defect Ageing Matrix</h3><span class="sub">open defects by severity × days-open · click a cell to drill</span></div>
        ${heatmap(matrix)}<div id="ageDrillBox"></div></div>`
    + `<div class="grid g-2 mt">
        <div class="card"><div class="card-h"><h3>Age Distribution</h3><span class="sub">open defects</span></div>
          ${barChart('ageDist',dist.map(b=>b.k+'d'),[{label:'Open defects',data:dist.map(b=>b.n),backgroundColor:[CFG.color.ok,CFG.color.info,CFG.color.warn,CFG.color.orange,CFG.color.danger]}],'h240')}</div>
        <div class="card"><div class="card-h"><h3>Oldest Open Defects</h3><span class="sub">${opens.length} open</span></div>
          ${oldest.map(oldRow).join('')||'<div class="empty">'+ico(ICONS.check)+'<br>No open defects — all resolved</div>'}</div>
      </div>`;
};
function ageDrill(sev,i){ const b=ageDrill._data[sev]&&ageDrill._data[sev][i]; const box=$('#ageDrillBox'); if(!box||!b)return;
  const items=[...b.items].sort((a,b)=>b.age-a.age);
  box.innerHTML=`<div class="dsection">${esc(sev)} severity · ${b.k} days open · ${b.n} defect(s)</div>`+
    (items.map(x=>`<div class="linkrow" onclick="openDrawer('defect','${esc(x.code)}')">
      <span class="code" style="color:var(--orange)">${esc(x.code)}</span><span class="lt">${esc(x.description)}</span>
      <span class="pill neutral">${x.age}d</span></div>`).join('')||'<div class="empty">None</div>');
  box.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'nearest'});
}

/* ================= RISK & DELAY ================= */
Views.risk=function(){
  const health=Engine.portfolioHealth();
  const roll=Engine.reqRollup().filter(x=>!x.req.parked).sort((a,b)=>b.risk.score-a.risk.score);
  const AS=Store.asOf;
  const delayedReq=Store.req.filter(r=>r.isOverdue).sort((a,b)=>b.overdue-a.overdue);
  const delayedStory=Store.stories.filter(s=>s.isOverdue).sort((a,b)=>b.overdue-a.overdue);
  const blocked=Store.stories.filter(s=>s.blocked);
  const dueWeek=Store.stories.filter(s=>!s.done&&s.due&&daysBetween(AS,s.due)>=0&&daysBetween(AS,s.due)<=7);
  const kpis=[
    kpiCard({label:'Portfolio Health',val:health.score,valColor:health.score>=75?'var(--ok)':health.score>=50?'var(--warn)':'var(--danger)',color:health.score>=75?'green':health.score>=50?'warn':'danger',icon:'check',bar:health.score,sub:`<span class="pill ${health.band==='Healthy'?'ok':health.band==='At Risk'?'warn':'danger'}">${health.band}</span>`}),
    kpiCard({label:'High-Risk Requirements',val:roll.filter(x=>x.risk.score>=60).length,color:'danger',icon:'risk'}),
    kpiCard({label:'Overdue Items',val:delayedReq.length+delayedStory.length,color:'orange',icon:'alert'}),
    kpiCard({label:'Blocked Work',val:blocked.length,color:'danger',icon:'block'}),
    kpiCard({label:'Due This Week',val:dueWeek.length,color:'warn',icon:'clock'}),
  ].join('');
  const riskCols=[
    {h:'Requirement',key:'code',sortVal:r=>r.req.code,render:r=>reqTrackLink(r.req.id,r.req.code)},
    {h:'Subject',key:'subject',cls:'tt',sortVal:r=>r.req.subject,render:r=>esc(r.req.subject)},
    {h:'Priority',key:'pri',sortVal:r=>PRIORITY_RANK[r.req.priority]||0,render:r=>priPill(r.req.priority)},
    {h:'Risk Factors',key:'f',render:r=>r.risk.factors.slice(0,2).map(f=>`<span class="pill neutral" style="margin-right:4px">${esc(f[0])}</span>`).join('')||'<span class="dim">—</span>'},
    {h:'Risk Score',key:'score',cls:'tnum',sortVal:r=>r.risk.score,render:r=>`<div class="pwrap"><div class="pbar" style="width:70px"><span style="width:${r.risk.score}%;background:${r.risk.score>=60?'var(--danger)':r.risk.score>=35?'var(--warn)':'var(--ok)'}"></span></div><span class="pval">${r.risk.score}</span></div>`},
  ];
  const rrows=roll.map(r=>({...r,_kind:'req',id:r.req.id}));
  const delayRow=(x,kind)=>`<div class="linkrow" onclick="openDrawer('${kind}','${esc(keyOf(x.id))}')">
    <span class="code">${esc(x.code)}</span><span class="lt">${esc(x.subject)}</span>
    <span class="pill danger">${x.overdue}d late</span></div>`;
  return pageHead('Risk & Delay Dashboard','Risk scoring, schedule slippage, and blockers')
    + `<div class="grid g-kpi">${kpis}</div>`
    + `<div class="grid g-3 mt">
        <div class="card"><div class="card-h"><h3>Portfolio Health</h3></div>
          ${gaugeSVG(health.score,health.band)}
          <div class="muted" style="text-align:center;font-size:12px;margin-top:6px">${health.atRisk} of ${health.total} requirements below healthy threshold</div></div>
        <div class="card"><div class="card-h"><h3>Delayed Requirements</h3><span class="sub">${delayedReq.length}</span></div>
          ${delayedReq.slice(0,7).map(x=>delayRow(x,'req')).join('')||'<div class="empty">'+ico(ICONS.check)+'<br>Nothing overdue</div>'}</div>
        <div class="card"><div class="card-h"><h3>Blocked Stories</h3><span class="sub">${blocked.length}</span></div>
          ${blocked.slice(0,7).map(x=>`<div class="linkrow" onclick="openDrawer('story','${esc(x.code)}')"><span class="code">${esc(x.code)}</span><span class="lt">${esc(x.subject)}</span><span class="pill danger">Blocked</span></div>`).join('')||'<div class="empty">'+ico(ICONS.check)+'<br>None blocked</div>'}</div>
      </div>`
    + `<div class="card mt"><div class="card-h"><h3>Requirement Risk Ranking</h3><span class="sub">weighted: overdue, critical defects, blockers, priority, schedule</span></div>
        ${dataTable('riskTable',riskCols,rrows,'openDrawer')}</div>`;
};

/* ================= REQUIREMENT · USER STORY TRACKING (detail screen) ================= */
Views.reqTrack=function(id){
  const r=Store.byReqCode.get(id)||Store.byReqId.get(keyOf(id));
  if(!r) return `<button class="tbtn" onclick="Router.go('requirements')">← Back to Requirements</button>
    <div class="empty" style="margin-top:20px">Requirement not found</div>`;
  const ss=Engine.storyStats(r.stories), risk=Engine.riskScore(r), ext=Ext.url('req',r.id,r.code);
  const kpis=[
    kpiCard({label:'User Stories',val:ss.t,color:'blue',icon:'story'}),
    kpiCard({label:'Completed',val:ss.done+'<small>/'+ss.t+'</small>',color:'green',icon:'check',bar:ss.pctDone}),
    kpiCard({label:'Avg Completion',val:ss.comp+'<small>%</small>',color:'brand',icon:'exec',bar:ss.comp}),
    kpiCard({label:'Total Plan/Actual Hrs',val:ss.totalA+'<small>/'+ss.totalP+'h</small>',color:'info',icon:'clock',bar:ss.hoursPct}),
    kpiCard({label:'This Wk Plan/Actual',val:ss.weekA+'<small>/'+ss.weekP+'h</small>',color:'violet',icon:'clock',bar:ss.weekPct}),
    kpiCard({label:'Blocked / QA / Deploy',val:ss.blocked+' / '+ss.qa+' / '+ss.dep,color:ss.blocked?'danger':'warn',icon:'deploy'}),
  ].join('');
  // detailed tracking columns (full SDLC timeline)
  const cols=[
    {h:'Story',key:'code',render:s=>codeLink('story',s.id,s.code)},
    {h:'Subject',key:'subject',cls:'tt',render:s=>esc(s.subject)},
    {h:'Stage',key:'stage',sortVal:s=>s.stageIdx,render:s=>stagePill(s.stage)},
    {h:'Status',key:'status',render:s=>pillFor('story',s)},
    {h:'Owner',key:'owner'},
    {h:'Developer',key:'devBy',render:s=>esc(s.devBy)},
    {h:'Total Plan',key:'totalPlanned',cls:'tnum',sortVal:s=>s.totalPlanned==null?-1:s.totalPlanned,render:s=>s.totalPlanned!=null?`<span class="mono" style="color:var(--info)">${s.totalPlanned}h</span>`:'<span class="dim">—</span>'},
    {h:'Wk Plan',key:'weekPlanned',cls:'tnum',sortVal:r=>r.weekPlanned==null?-1:r.weekPlanned,render:r=>r.weekPlanned!=null?`<span class="mono" style="color:var(--violet)">${r.weekPlanned}h</span>`:'<span class="dim">\u2014</span>'},
    {h:'Wk Actual',key:'weekActual',cls:'tnum',sortVal:r=>r.weekActual==null?-1:r.weekActual,render:r=>{
        if(r.weekActual==null) return '<span class="dim">\u2014</span>';
        const over=r.weekPlanned!=null&&r.weekActual>r.weekPlanned;
        return `<span class="mono" style="color:${over?'var(--danger)':'var(--brand)'}">${r.weekActual}h</span>`; }},
    {h:'Total Actual',key:'totalActual',cls:'tnum',sortVal:s=>s.totalActual==null?-1:s.totalActual,render:s=>{
        if(s.totalActual==null) return '<span class="dim">—</span>';
        const over=s.totalPlanned!=null&&s.totalActual>s.totalPlanned;
        return `<span class="mono" style="color:${over?'var(--danger)':'var(--ok)'}">${s.totalActual}h</span>`;
      }},
    {h:'Build',key:'buildStart',sortVal:s=>s.buildStart?+pd(s.buildStart):0,render:s=>`<span class="mono dim">${fmtShort(s.buildStart)}→${fmtShort(s.buildComplete)}</span>`},
    {h:'QA',key:'qaComplete',sortVal:s=>s.qaComplete?+pd(s.qaComplete):0,render:s=>`<span class="mono dim">${fmtShort(s.qaComplete)}</span>`},
    {h:'UAT',key:'uatComplete',sortVal:s=>s.uatComplete?+pd(s.uatComplete):0,render:s=>`<span class="mono dim">${fmtShort(s.uatComplete)}</span>`},
    {h:'Deployed',key:'deployDate',sortVal:s=>s.deployDate?+pd(s.deployDate):0,render:s=>s.deployDate?`<span class="mono" style="color:var(--ok)">${fmtShort(s.deployDate)}</span>`:'—'},
    {h:'Completion',key:'comp',render:s=>progress(s.comp,s.done?'var(--ok)':s.blocked?'var(--danger)':'var(--brand)')},
    {h:'Due',key:'due',sortVal:s=>s.due?+pd(s.due):Infinity,render:s=>s.isOverdue?`<span class="pill danger">${s.overdue}d late</span>`:fmtShort(s.due)},
  ];
  const defCols=[
    {h:'Defect',key:'code',render:d=>codeLink('defect',d.id,d.code)},
    {h:'Description',key:'description',cls:'tt',render:d=>esc(d.description)},
    {h:'Severity',key:'severity',render:d=>sevPill(d.severity)},
    {h:'Story',key:'storyCode',render:d=>fkCodeLink('story',d.storyCode,'var(--blue)')},
    {h:'Status',key:'status',render:d=>pillFor('defect',d)},
    {h:'Age',key:'age',cls:'tnum',render:d=>`<span class="mono">${d.age}d</span>`},
  ];
  return `<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;margin-bottom:16px;flex-wrap:wrap">
      <button class="tbtn" onclick="Router.go('requirements')">← Requirements</button>
      ${reqTrackLink && ext?`<a class="tbtn" href="${esc(ext)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path d="M18 13v6a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V8a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h6M15 3h6v6M10 14 21 3"/></svg>Open in source</a>`:''}
    </div>
    <div class="page-head"><div>
      <h2>${codeLink('req',r.id,r.code)} <span style="color:var(--ink-3);font-weight:600">·</span> ${esc(r.subject)}</h2>
      <p>User Story delivery tracking · ${esc(r.module)} · ${esc(nz(r.type))}</p></div>
      <div>${pillFor('req',r)} ${priPill(r.priority)}</div>
    </div>
    ${r.idCollision?`<div class="card" style="margin-bottom:14px;border-color:var(--warn);background:rgba(240,180,41,.08)">
      <div style="display:flex;gap:11px;align-items:flex-start">
        <span style="color:var(--warn);flex-shrink:0">${ico(ICONS.alert)}</span>
        <div style="font-size:12.5px;line-height:1.55">
          <b style="color:var(--warn)">Source ID precision warning.</b> In the Excel export, this requirement's internal ID is stored as a number and is indistinguishable from ${r.collisionCodes.map(esc).join(', ')}. Because user stories link by that numeric ID, the stories listed below may include stories that actually belong to those requirements. To fix permanently, re-export the <b>Id</b>, <b>Requirement</b>, <b>Parent User Story</b>, <b>Associated Req</b> and <b>Associated Story</b> columns as <b>Text</b>, then use <b>Load Excel</b>.
        </div></div></div>`:''}
    <div class="card" style="margin-bottom:14px"><div class="dgrid" style="margin-bottom:6px">
      <div class="drow"><span class="dl2">Requirement Completion</span><span class="dv2">${compPill(r.comp)}</span></div>
      <div class="drow"><span class="dl2">Owner</span><span class="dv2">${esc(r.owner)}</span></div>
      <div class="drow"><span class="dl2">Risk Score</span><span class="dv2"><span class="pill ${risk.score>=60?'danger':risk.score>=35?'warn':'ok'}">${risk.score}</span></span></div>
      <div class="drow"><span class="dl2">Due</span><span class="dv2">${r.isOverdue?`<span class="pill danger">${r.overdue}d late</span>`:fmtDate(r.due)}</span></div>
    </div>
    <div style="margin-top:6px">${progress(r.comp,r.done?'var(--ok)':'var(--brand)')}</div></div>
    <div class="grid g-kpi">${kpis}</div>
    <div class="mt">${compCard('reqtrack','Story Completion Breakdown',r.stories)}</div>
    <div class="card mt">${(function(){
        const stages=[...new Set(r.stories.map(s=>s.stage).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>CFG.stageOrder.indexOf(a)-CFG.stageOrder.indexOf(b));
        const statuses=[...new Set(r.stories.map(s=>s.status||'(blank)'))];
        let list=r.stories;
        if(_trackStage) list=list.filter(s=>s.stage===_trackStage);
        if(_trackStatus) list=list.filter(s=>(s.status||'(blank)')===_trackStatus);
        const opt=(v,cur)=>`<option value="${esc(v)}" ${v===(cur||'')?'selected':''}>${esc(v||'All')}</option>`;
        const stageSel=['',...stages].map(v=>opt(v,_trackStage)).join('');
        const statSel=['',...statuses].map(v=>opt(v,_trackStatus)).join('');
        return `<div class="card-h"><h3>User Story Tracking</h3><span class="sub">${list.length} of ${r.stories.length} stories · full SDLC timeline · click a row for detail</span></div>
        <div class="filterbar" style="margin-bottom:14px">
          <div class="fsel"><label>Stage</label><select onchange="setTrackStage(this.value)">${stageSel}</select></div>
          <div class="fsel"><label>Status</label><select onchange="setTrackStatus(this.value)">${statSel}</select></div>
          ${(_trackStage||_trackStatus)?`<button class="clearf" onclick="clearTrackFilter()">✕ Clear</button>`:''}
        </div>
        ${dataTable('trackTable',cols,list,'openDrawer')}`;
      })()}</div>
    ${r.defects.length?`<div class="card mt"><div class="card-h"><h3>Linked Defects</h3><span class="sub">${r.defects.length}</span></div>
      ${dataTable('trackDef',defCols,r.defects,'openDrawer')}</div>`:''}`;
};
function goReqTrack(code){ const _r=Store.byReqCode.get(code)||Store.byReqId.get(keyOf(code)); Router.param=_r?_r.code:code; Router.current='reqTrack'; _stageFilter=null; _weekFilter=null; _trackStage=null; _trackStatus=null;
  closeDrawer(); buildNav();
  const r=Store.byReqCode.get(Router.param)||Store.byReqId.get(keyOf(code));
  $('#crumb').innerHTML=`<span class="dim" style="cursor:pointer" onclick="Router.go('requirements')">Requirements</span> › <b>${r?esc(r.code):esc(code)}</b>`;
  Router.refresh(); $('#main').scrollTop=0;
  if(window.innerWidth<=900)$('#rail').classList.remove('open');
}

/* =====================================================================================
   7) DRILL-DOWN — detail drawer
   ===================================================================================== */
function openDrawer(kind,id){
  const map={req:Store.byReqId,story:Store.byStoryId};
  let x = kind==='req'?(Store.byReqCode.get(id)||Store.byReqId.get(keyOf(id)))
        : kind==='story'?(Store.byStoryCode.get(id)||Store.byStoryId.get(keyOf(id)))
        : (Store.defects.find(d=>d.code===id)||Store.defects.find(d=>keyOf(d.id)===keyOf(id)));
  if(!x)return;
  const d=$('#drawer'); let html='';
  const F=(l,v)=>v?`<div class="dfield"><div class="dl">${esc(l)}</div><div class="dv">${esc(v)}</div></div>`:'';
  const R=(l,v)=>`<div class="drow"><span class="dl2">${esc(l)}</span><span class="dv2">${v}</span></div>`;

  if(kind==='req'){
    const ss=Engine.storyStats(x.stories), risk=Engine.riskScore(x);
    html=`<div class="drawer-h"><div><div>${codeLink('req',x.id,x.code)} <span class="dim">· ${esc(x.module)}</span></div>
      <h3>${esc(x.subject)}</h3></div><button class="x" onclick="closeDrawer()">✕</button></div>
      <div class="drawer-b">
      <div style="margin-bottom:16px">${progress(x.comp,x.done?'var(--ok)':'var(--brand)')}</div>
      <button class="tbtn" style="margin-bottom:16px" onclick="goReqTrack('${esc(x.code)}')">
        <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path d="M3 3v18h18"/><path d="m7 14 4-4 3 3 5-6"/></svg>Open full user-story tracking screen</button>
      <div class="dgrid">
        ${R('Status',pillFor('req',x))}${R('Priority',priPill(x.priority))}
        ${R('Type',esc(nz(x.type)))}${R('Complexity',esc(nz(x.complexity)))}
        ${R('Owner',esc(x.owner))}${R('Risk Score',`<span class="pill ${risk.score>=60?'danger':risk.score>=35?'warn':'ok'}">${risk.score}</span>`)}
        ${R('Start',fmtDate(x.startDate))}${R('Expected',fmtDate(x.expectedDate))}
      </div>
      ${x.isOverdue?`<div class="dfield"><span class="pill danger">⚠ Overdue by ${x.overdue} days</span></div>`:''}
      ${risk.factors.length?`<div class="dfield"><div class="dl">Risk Factors</div>${risk.factors.map(f=>`<span class="pill neutral" style="margin:2px 4px 2px 0">${esc(f[0])} (+${Math.round(f[1])})</span>`).join('')}</div>`:''}
      ${F('Requirement',x.requirement)}${F('Business Value',x.businessValue)}${F('Proposed Solution',x.solution)}${F('User Benefits',x.userBenefits)}${F('Remarks',x.remarks)}
      <div class="dsection">Linked User Stories (${x.stories.length}) · ${ss.done} done · ${ss.comp}% avg · ${ss.cons}/${ss.est} pts</div>
      ${x.stories.map(s=>`<div class="linkrow" onclick="openDrawer('story','${esc(s.code)}')">
        <span class="code" style="color:var(--blue)">${esc(s.code)}</span><span class="lt">${esc(s.subject)}</span>${stagePill(s.stage)}${compPill(s.comp)}</div>`).join('')||'<div class="dim" style="font-size:12px">No linked stories</div>'}
      <div class="dsection">Linked Defects (${x.defects.length})</div>
      ${x.defects.map(df=>`<div class="linkrow" onclick="openDrawer('defect','${esc(df.code)}')">
        <span class="code" style="color:var(--orange)">${esc(df.code)}</span><span class="lt">${esc(df.description)}</span>${sevPill(df.severity)}</div>`).join('')||'<div class="dim" style="font-size:12px">No linked defects</div>'}
      </div>`;
  }
  else if(kind==='story'){
    const stages=[['Build Start',x.buildStart],['Build Done',x.buildComplete],['QA Done',x.qaComplete],['UAT Done',x.uatComplete],['Deployed',x.deployDate]];
    html=`<div class="drawer-h"><div><div>${codeLink('story',x.id,x.code)}${x.reqCode?' <span class="dim">· </span>'+fkCodeLink('req',x.reqCode,'var(--violet)'):''}</div>
      <h3>${esc(x.subject)}</h3></div><button class="x" onclick="closeDrawer()">✕</button></div>
      <div class="drawer-b">
      <div style="margin-bottom:16px">${progress(x.comp,x.done?'var(--ok)':x.blocked?'var(--danger)':'var(--brand)')}</div>
      <div class="dgrid">
        ${R('Stage',stagePill(x.stage))}${R('Status',pillFor('story',x))}
        ${R('Owner',esc(x.owner))}${R('Developed By',esc(x.devBy))}
        ${R('QA Owner',esc(x.qaOwner))}${R('Points',`<span class="mono">${x.cons}/${x.est} (${x.rem} left)</span>`)}
        ${R('Week',x.weekNo!=null?'W'+x.weekNo:'—')}${R('TDD',esc(nz(x.tdd)))}
        ${R('Planned Hours',x.plannedHours!=null?x.plannedHours+'h':'—')}${R('Actual Hours',x.actualHours!=null?`<span style="color:${x.plannedHours!=null&&x.actualHours>x.plannedHours?'var(--danger)':'var(--ok)'}">${x.actualHours}h</span>`:'—')}
      </div>
      ${x.blocked?`<div class="dfield"><span class="pill danger">⛔ Blocked / waiting on dependency</span></div>`:''}
      ${x.isOverdue?`<div class="dfield"><span class="pill danger">⚠ Overdue by ${x.overdue} days</span></div>`:''}
      <div class="dsection">Delivery Timeline</div>
      ${stages.map(([l,v])=>`<div class="drow"><span class="dl2">${l}</span><span class="dv2 mono">${fmtDate(v)}</span></div>`).join('')}
      ${F('Description',x.description)}${F('Acceptance Criteria',x.acceptance)}${F('Tech Notes',x.techNotes)}
      ${x.parent?`<div class="dsection">Parent Story</div><div class="linkrow" onclick="openDrawer('story','${esc(x.parent.code)}')"><span class="code" style="color:var(--blue)">${esc(x.parent.code)}</span><span class="lt">${esc(x.parent.subject)}</span>${compPill(x.parent.comp)}</div>`:''}
      ${x.children&&x.children.length?`<div class="dsection">Child Stories (${x.children.length})</div>`+x.children.map(c=>`<div class="linkrow" onclick="openDrawer('story','${esc(c.code)}')"><span class="code" style="color:var(--blue)">${esc(c.code)}</span><span class="lt">${esc(c.subject)}</span>${compPill(c.comp)}</div>`).join(''):''}
      ${x.reqCode?(()=>{const rq=Store.byReqId.get(keyOf(x.reqFk));return `<div class="dsection">Parent Requirement</div><div class="linkrow" onclick="openDrawer('req','${esc(x.reqCode)}')"><span class="code" style="color:var(--violet)">${esc(x.reqCode)}</span><span class="lt">${esc(x.reqSubject||'')}</span>${rq?compPill(rq.comp):''}</div>`;})():''}
      </div>`;
  }
  else {
    html=`<div class="drawer-h"><div><div>${codeLink('defect',x.id,x.code)}</div>
      <h3>${esc(x.description||'Defect')}</h3></div><button class="x" onclick="closeDrawer()">✕</button></div>
      <div class="drawer-b">
      <div class="dgrid">
        ${R('Severity',sevPill(x.severity))}${R('Status',pillFor('defect',x))}
        ${R('Category',esc(nz(x.category)))}${R('Environment',esc(nz(x.environment)))}
        ${R('Owner',esc(x.owner))}${R('Fixed By',esc(x.fixedBy))}
        ${R('Reported',fmtDate(x.reportedDate))}${R('Age',`<span class="mono">${x.age} days</span>`)}
      </div>
      ${F('Description',x.description)}${F('Root Cause',x.rootCause)}${F('Fix Details',x.fixDetails)}${F('Dependency',x.dependency)}
      ${x.reqCode?`<div class="dsection">Linked Requirement</div><div class="linkrow" onclick="openDrawer('req','${esc(x.reqCode)}')"><span class="code" style="color:var(--violet)">${esc(x.reqCode)}</span></div>`:''}
      ${x.storyCode?`<div class="dsection">Linked Story</div><div class="linkrow" onclick="openDrawer('story','${esc(x.storyCode)}')"><span class="code" style="color:var(--blue)">${esc(x.storyCode)}</span></div>`:''}
      </div>`;
  }
  d.innerHTML=html; d.classList.add('open'); $('#scrim').classList.add('open');
}
function closeDrawer(){ $('#drawer').classList.remove('open'); $('#scrim').classList.remove('open'); }

/* =====================================================================================
   8) EXPORT — CSV / Excel / PDF
   ===================================================================================== */
const Export={
  flatReq:()=>Store.req.map(r=>({ID:r.code,Subject:r.subject,Module:r.module,Type:r.type,Priority:r.priority,
    Complexity:r.complexity,Status:r.status,'Completion %':r.comp,Owner:r.owner,Start:r.startDate,
    Expected:r.expectedDate,'Revised Delivery':r.revisedDate,Remarks:r.remarks,'Overdue Days':r.overdue,Stories:r.stories.length,Defects:r.defects.length})),
  flatStory:()=>Store.stories.map(s=>({ID:s.code,Story:s.subject,Requirement:s.reqCode,Stage:s.stage,Status:s.status,
    Owner:s.owner,'Developed By':s.devBy,'Points Est':s.est,'Points Consumed':s.cons,'Points Rem':s.rem,
    'Completion %':s.comp,Week:s.weekNo,'Total Plan Hrs':s.totalPlanned,'Total Actual Hrs':s.totalActual,'Wk Planned':s.weekPlanned,'Wk Actual':s.weekActual,'Build Start':s.buildStart,Deployed:s.deployDate,'Overdue Days':s.overdue})),
  flatDefect:()=>Store.defects.map(d=>({ID:d.code,Description:d.description,Severity:d.severity,Status:d.status,
    Category:d.category,Environment:d.environment,Requirement:d.reqCode,Story:d.storyCode,Owner:d.owner,
    'Fixed By':d.fixedBy,Reported:d.reportedDate,'Age Days':d.age})),
  currentEntity(){ const v=Router.current;
    if(v==='requirements'||v==='project')return{name:'Requirements',rows:this.flatReq()};
    if(v==='stories')return{name:'User_Stories',rows:this.flatStory()};
    if(v==='defects')return{name:'Defects',rows:this.flatDefect()};
    if(v==='dev')return{name:'Productivity',rows:Engine.productivity().map(p=>({Member:p.name,Owned:p.owned,Completed:p.done,Blocked:p.blocked,'Points Done':p.ptsDone,'Defects Fixed':p.defectsFixed,'Avg Cycle':p.avgCycle}))};
    if(v==='weekly')return{name:'Weekly',rows:Engine.weekly().map(w=>({Week:'W'+w.wk,Start:w.start,Planned:w.planned,Completed:w.done,Blocked:w.blocked,Points:w.ptsDone,Deployed:w.deployed}))};
    return{name:'Requirements',rows:this.flatReq()}; },
  toCSV(rows){ if(!rows.length)return''; const cols=Object.keys(rows[0]);
    const esc=v=>{v=v==null?'':String(v); return /[",\n]/.test(v)?'"'+v.replace(/"/g,'""')+'"':v;};
    return cols.join(',')+'\n'+rows.map(r=>cols.map(c=>esc(r[c])).join(',')).join('\n'); },
  download(name,content,type){ const b=new Blob([content],{type}); const u=URL.createObjectURL(b);
    const a=el('a'); a.href=u; a.download=name; a.click(); URL.revokeObjectURL(u); },
  csv(){ const e=this.currentEntity(); this.download(e.name+'.csv',this.toCSV(e.rows),'text/csv'); toast('Exported '+e.name+'.csv'); },
  excel(){ if(typeof XLSX==='undefined'){toast('Excel library unavailable');return;}
    const wb=XLSX.utils.book_new();
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb,XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(this.flatReq()),'Requirements');
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb,XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(this.flatStory()),'User Stories');
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb,XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(this.flatDefect()),'Defects');
    XLSX.writeFile(wb,'SDLC_Dashboard_Export.xlsx'); toast('Exported workbook (3 sheets)'); },
  pdf(){ closeDrawer(); toast('Opening print dialog — choose “Save as PDF”'); setTimeout(()=>window.print(),300); }
};
function exportMenu(btn){
  let m=$('#exportMenu'); if(m){m.remove();return;}
  m=el('div'); m.id='exportMenu';
  m.style.cssText='position:fixed;background:var(--panel-2);border:1px solid var(--line-2);border-radius:10px;box-shadow:var(--shadow);z-index:120;padding:5px;min-width:190px';
  const r=btn.getBoundingClientRect(); m.style.top=(r.bottom+6)+'px'; m.style.right=(window.innerWidth-r.right)+'px';
  const item=(label,fn)=>{const b=el('button',null,label); b.style.cssText='display:block;width:100%;text-align:left;background:none;border:none;color:var(--ink);padding:9px 12px;border-radius:7px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600';
    b.onmouseover=()=>b.style.background='var(--elev)'; b.onmouseout=()=>b.style.background='none';
    b.onclick=()=>{m.remove();fn();}; return b;};
  m.append(item('⬇  Current view → CSV',()=>Export.csv()),item('⬇  Full workbook → Excel',()=>Export.excel()),item('🖨  This dashboard → PDF',()=>Export.pdf()));
  document.body.append(m);
  setTimeout(()=>document.addEventListener('click',function h(e){if(!m.contains(e.target)&&e.target!==btn){m.remove();document.removeEventListener('click',h);}}),0);
}

/* ---- record hyperlink settings ---- */
function openLinkSettings(){
  let ov=document.getElementById('linkModal'); if(ov)ov.remove();
  ov=el('div'); ov.id='linkModal';
  ov.style.cssText='position:fixed;inset:0;background:rgba(4,6,10,.62);backdrop-filter:blur(2px);z-index:150;display:grid;place-items:center;padding:20px';
  const field=(k,label,ph)=>`<div class="dfield"><div class="dl">${label}</div>
    <input id="lt_${k}" value="${esc(Ext.tpl[k]||'')}" placeholder="${esc(ph)}" spellcheck="false"
      style="width:100%;background:var(--panel);border:1px solid var(--line);color:var(--ink);padding:9px 11px;border-radius:8px;font-size:12px;font-family:var(--mono);outline:none"></div>`;
  ov.innerHTML=`<div class="card" style="width:min(640px,96vw);max-height:90vh;overflow:auto" onclick="event.stopPropagation()">
    <div class="card-h"><h3>Record Hyperlinks</h3><button class="x" onclick="document.getElementById('linkModal').remove()">✕</button></div>
    <p class="muted" style="font-size:12.5px;margin:0 0 16px;line-height:1.6">Paste the URL pattern your source system (e.g. Zoho) uses for each record type. Put <b class="mono" style="color:var(--brand)">{id}</b> where the record's numeric ID goes (and optionally <b class="mono" style="color:var(--brand)">{code}</b> for the SDLC code). Every ID in the tables and detail panels then opens that record in a new tab.</p>
    ${field('req','SDLC Requirement URL pattern','https://crm.zoho.com/crm/org000000/tab/CustomModule/{id}')}
    ${field('story','SDLC User Story URL pattern','https://crm.zoho.com/crm/org000000/tab/CustomModule/{id}')}
    ${field('defect','SDLC Defect URL pattern','https://crm.zoho.com/crm/org000000/tab/CustomModule/{id}')}
    <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;justify-content:flex-end;margin-top:6px">
      <button class="tbtn" onclick="clearLinkSettings()">Clear</button>
      <button class="tbtn" onclick="document.getElementById('linkModal').remove()">Cancel</button>
      <button class="tbtn primary" onclick="saveLinkSettings()">Save links</button>
    </div>
    <p class="dim" style="font-size:11px;margin:14px 0 0;line-height:1.55">Tip: open any one record in your browser, copy its URL from the address bar, paste it here, and replace the long number with <span class="mono" style="color:var(--ink-2)">{id}</span>. Settings are saved in this browser.</p>
  </div>`;
  ov.onclick=()=>ov.remove();
  document.body.append(ov);
}
function saveLinkSettings(){ ['req','story','defect'].forEach(k=>{ const i=document.getElementById('lt_'+k); if(i)Ext.tpl[k]=i.value.trim(); });
  Ext.save(); const ov=document.getElementById('linkModal'); if(ov)ov.remove();
  const open=$('#drawer').classList.contains('open'); Router.refresh();
  toast(Ext.has()?'Record links saved — IDs are now clickable':'Record links cleared'); }
function clearLinkSettings(){ ['req','story','defect'].forEach(k=>{ const i=document.getElementById('lt_'+k); if(i)i.value=''; }); }

/* ---- live Excel re-import (same normalization pipeline) ---- */
function ingestExcelFile(file){
  const reader=new FileReader();
  reader.onload=e=>{ try{
    const wb=XLSX.read(e.target.result,{type:'array',cellDates:true});
    const ws=wb.Sheets[wb.SheetNames[0]];
    const rows=XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(ws,{defval:null,raw:true});
    if(!rows.length){toast('No rows found');return;}
    const H=Object.keys(rows[0]);
    const iso=v=>v instanceof Date?v.toISOString().slice(0,10):(v||null);
    const fid=v=>v==null?null:(typeof v==='number'?v.toFixed(0):String(v));
    const S=v=>{v=v==null?null:String(v).trim(); return v===''?null:v;};
    if(H.includes('SDLC Req Id')){
      Store.raw.requirements=rows.map(r=>({id:fid(r['Id']),code:S(r['SDLC Req Id']),subject:S(r['Subject']),
        module:S(r['Module']),type:S(r['Type']),priority:S(r['Priority']),complexity:S(r['Complexity']),
        status:S(r['Req Status']),completion:r['Completion %'],startDate:iso(r['Start Date']),
        expectedDate:iso(r['Expected Delivery Date']),revisedDate:iso(r['Revised delivery date']),
        ownerId:fid(r['SDLC Req Owner']),owner:S(r['SDLC Req Owner Name']),requirement:S(r['Requirement']),
        businessValue:S(r['Business Value']),solution:S(r['Solution']),userBenefits:S(r['User Benefits']),
        remarks:S(r['Remarks']),
        createdTime:iso(r['Created Time']),modifiedTime:iso(r['Modified Time']),lastActivity:iso(r['Last Activity Time'])}));
      toast('Requirements reloaded ('+rows.length+' rows)');
    } else if(H.includes('User Story')){
      Store.raw.stories=rows.map(r=>({id:fid(r['Id']),code:S(r['User Story']),subject:S(r['Story Subject']),
        reqFk:fid(r['Requirement']),parentFk:fid(r['Parent User Story']),pointsEst:r['Points Estimated'],
        pointsCons:r['Points Consumed'],pointsRem:r['Points Remaining'],stage:S(r['Stage']),status:S(r['Status']),
        totalPlanned:r['TotalPlanned Hours']||r['Planned Hours'],totalActual:r['Total Actual Hours']||r['Actual Hours'],weekPlanned:r['Current Week Planned'],weekActual:r['Current Week Actual'],
        tdd:S(r['TDD Status']),ownerId:fid(r['SDLC User Story Owner']),owner:S(r['SDLC User Story Owner Name']),
        devById:fid(r['Developed By']),qaOwnerId:fid(r['QA Owner']),buildStart:iso(r['Build Start Date']),
        buildComplete:iso(r['Build Complete Date']),qaComplete:iso(r['QA Complete Date']),uatComplete:iso(r['UAT Complete Date']),
        demoDate:iso(r['Demo Date']),deployDate:iso(r['Depoyment Date']),estEndDate:iso(r['Estimate End Date']),
        completion:r['Completion'],weekNo:r['Week NO'],wkStart:iso(r['Wk Start Date']),acceptance:S(r['Acceptance Criteria']),
        description:S(r['Description']),techNotes:S(r['Tech Solution / Comments']),
        createdTime:iso(r['Created Time']),modifiedTime:iso(r['Modified Time']),lastActivity:iso(r['Last Activity Time'])}));
      toast('User stories reloaded ('+rows.length+' rows)');
    } else if(H.includes('SDLC Defects Name')){
      Store.raw.defects=rows.map(r=>({id:fid(r['Id']),code:S(r['SDLC Defects Name']),reqFk:fid(r['Associated Req']),
        storyFk:fid(r['Associated Story']),status:S(r['Defect Status']),severity:S(r['Severity']),category:S(r['Defect Category']),
        environment:S(r['Environment']),ownerId:fid(r['SDLC Defects Owner']),owner:S(r['SDLC Defects Owner Name']),
        fixedById:fid(r['Fixed By']),description:S(r['Defect Description']),rootCause:S(r['Root Cause']),
        fixDetails:S(r['Fix Details']),fixSize:r['Fix Size'],dependency:S(r['Dependency']),reportedDate:iso(r['Date']),
        createdTime:iso(r['Created Time']),modifiedTime:iso(r['Modified Time']),lastActivity:iso(r['Last Activity Time'])}));
      toast('Defects reloaded ('+rows.length+' rows)');
    } else { toast('Unrecognized sheet — expected the SDLC export format'); return; }
    // merge people from any owner-name columns present
    rows.forEach(r=>{ [['SDLC Req Owner','SDLC Req Owner Name'],['SDLC User Story Owner','SDLC User Story Owner Name'],['SDLC Defects Owner','SDLC Defects Owner Name']].forEach(([i,n])=>{ if(r[i]&&r[n])Store.raw.people[fid(r[i])]=r[n]; }); });
    Store.ingest(Store.raw); rebuildDims(); Router.refresh();
  }catch(err){ console.error(err); toast('Could not read file: '+err.message); } };
  reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
}
function rebuildDims(){ $('#asOfLabel').textContent=fmtDate(Store.asOf);
  $('#railFoot').innerHTML=`${Store.req.length} reqs · ${Store.stories.length} stories · ${Store.defects.length} defects`; buildNav(); }

/* =====================================================================================
   9) NAV + SEARCH + ROUTER + INIT
   ===================================================================================== */
const NAV=[
  {g:'Delivery'},
  {id:'requirements',label:'Requirements',icon:'req',badge:()=>Store.req.length},
  {id:'stories',label:'User Stories',icon:'story',badge:()=>Store.stories.length},
  {id:'defects',label:'Defects',icon:'defect',badge:()=>Store.defects.length},
  {id:'age',label:'Defect Ageing',icon:'age',badge:()=>Store.defects.filter(d=>d.open).length},
];
function buildNav(){ const n=$('#nav'); n.innerHTML='';
  NAV.forEach(it=>{ if(it.g){ n.append(el('div','nav-group',it.g)); return; }
    const d=el('div','nav-item'+(Router.current===it.id?' active':''));
    d.innerHTML=ico(ICONS[it.icon])+`<span>${it.label}</span>`+(it.badge?`<span class="badge">${it.badge()}</span>`:'');
    d.onclick=()=>Router.go(it.id); n.append(d); }); }

const Router={ current:'requirements', param:null,
  go(id){ if(!Views[id])return; this.current=id; this.param=null; _stageFilter=null; _weekFilter=null; _hoursFilter=null;
    $('#crumb').innerHTML='<b>'+(NAV.find(x=>x.id===id)||{}).label+'</b>';
    if(window.innerWidth<=900)$('#rail').classList.remove('open');
    buildNav(); this.refresh(); $('#main').scrollTop=0; },
  refresh(){ Charts.clear();
    $('#main').innerHTML = this.current==='reqTrack'? Views.reqTrack(this.param) : Views[this.current]();
    const cc=$('#chipCount'); if(cc){ const n={requirements:WS.req().length,stories:WS.stories().length,defects:WS.defects().length}[this.current];
      if(n!=null)cc.innerHTML=`<b>${n}</b> shown`; } }
};

/* ---- global search ---- */
function runSearch(q){ const box=$('#searchResults'); q=q.trim().toLowerCase();
  if(q.length<2){box.classList.remove('open');return;}
  const hit=(x,kind,color)=>({x,kind,color,txt:x.subject||x.description||''});
  let res=[];
  Store.req.forEach(r=>{ if((r.code+' '+r.subject+' '+(r.requirement||'')).toLowerCase().includes(q))res.push({x:r,kind:'req',color:'var(--violet)'}); });
  Store.stories.forEach(s=>{ if((s.code+' '+s.subject+' '+(s.description||'')).toLowerCase().includes(q))res.push({x:s,kind:'story',color:'var(--blue)'}); });
  Store.defects.forEach(d=>{ if((d.code+' '+(d.description||'')).toLowerCase().includes(q))res.push({x:d,kind:'defect',color:'var(--orange)'}); });
  res=res.slice(0,20);
  box.innerHTML = res.length? res.map(r=>`<div class="sr-item" onclick="pickSearch('${r.kind}','${esc(r.x.code)}')">
    <span class="tag" style="background:${r.color}22;color:${r.color}">${esc(r.x.code)}</span>
    <span class="t">${esc(r.x.subject||r.x.description||'')}</span></div>`).join('')
    : '<div class="sr-item"><span class="t dim">No matches for “'+esc(q)+'”</span></div>';
  box.classList.add('open');
}
function pickSearch(kind,id){ $('#searchResults').classList.remove('open'); $('#globalSearch').value=''; openDrawer(kind,id); }

/* ---- INIT ---- */
function init(){
  Ext.load();
  let raw; try{ raw=JSON.parse($('#rawdata').textContent); }catch(e){ raw={requirements:[],stories:[],defects:[],people:{}}; }
  Store.ingest(raw); rebuildDims();
  Router.go('requirements');
  // events
  $('#globalSearch').addEventListener('input',debounce(e=>runSearch(e.target.value),160));
  $('#globalSearch').addEventListener('blur',()=>setTimeout(()=>$('#searchResults').classList.remove('open'),200));
  $('#fileInput').addEventListener('change',e=>{ if(e.target.files[0])ingestExcelFile(e.target.files[0]); e.target.value=''; });
  document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{ if(e.key==='/'&&document.activeElement.tagName!=='INPUT'){e.preventDefault();$('#globalSearch').focus();}
    if(e.key==='Escape'){closeDrawer();$('#searchResults').classList.remove('open');} });
  window.addEventListener('resize',()=>Router.refresh());
  $('#loader').style.display='none';
}
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',init);
</script>
</body>
</html>
